## Short PRAYERS.

### When the noble *Sophia* (or *eternal Wisdom*) kisses the *Soul* with her *Love*, and offers her *Love* to it.

> O MOST gracious and deep Love of GOD in *Christ Jesus*! I beseech you to grant me your *Pearl*, place it within my *Soul*, and take my *Soul* into your arms.
>
> O you sweet *Love*! I confess I am unclean before you. Take away my *uncleanliness* through *your death*, and carry the *hunger* and *thirst* of my soul through *your death* into *your resurrection* and *your triumph*! Cast my whole *selfhood* down to the ground in *your death*; take it *captive*, and carry my *hunger* through in your *hunger*.
>
> O highest *Love*, have you not appeared in me? Stay in me, and enclose me in you. Keep me in you, so that I may not be able to depart from you. Fill my *hunger* with your *love*, feed my soul with your *heavenly substance*, give it your *blood* to drink, and water it from your *fountain*.
>
> O great *Love*! Awaken my *disappeared image* in me, which, as to the kingdom of heaven, *disappeared* in my father *Adam*. By that *Word* which awakened the same *image* in the *Seed* of *the woman in Mary*, bring it to life, I beseech you.
>
> O you *life* and *power* of the Godhead, who has *promised* us, saying: "We will come to you, and make our home in you." O sweet *Love*! I bring my *desire* into this *word* of your *promise*. You have *promised* also, that *your Father will give the Holy Spirit to those who ask him for it*; therefore I now bring the *desire* of my soul into *that promise of yours*, and I receive your Word into my *hunger*. Increase in me my *hunger* for you. Strengthen me, O sweet *Love*, in your strength: bring me to life in you, that my spirit may taste your sweetness. O believe by your power in me, for *without you I can do nothing*.
>
> O sweet *Love*! I beseech you through that *Love* with which you overcame the *anger* of GOD, and changed it into *Love* and *divine joy*; I pray you, also change the *anger* in my soul by the same great *love*, so that I may become obedient to you, and that my soul may love you in it forever. O change my *will* into your *will*, bring your obedience into my disobedience, so that I may become obedient to you.
>
> O great *Love of Jesus Christ*, I humbly fly to you; bring the *hunger* of my soul into your *wounds*, from which you shed your holy *blood*, and quenched the *anger* with *love*.

may dwell: Let *my spirit* be the stringed instrument, harmony,
sound, and joy of *your Holy Spirit*. Strike the strings in me, in
your *regenerate image*, and carry my harmony into your
divine kingdom of *joy*, in the great *love* of GOD, in the wonders
of your glory and majesty, in the *communion* of the holy angelic
harmony. Build up the holy city *Zion* in me, in which as
*children* of Christ we all live together in *one city*, which is *Christ*
in us. Into you I wholly plunge myself; do with me what you
please. *Amen*.

---

# A PRAYER.

->*To be used by a soul in tribulation under the cross of
Christ, when it is assaulted by its outward enemies; who
persecute and hate it for being in the spirit of Christ,
and slander and reproach it as an evildoer.*<-

POOR man that I am; I walk full of anguish and trouble
in my return towards my native country, from which I
wandered in *Adam*, and am going back again through the
*thistles* and *thorns* of this troublesome *world*. O GOD
my Father, the *briers* tear me on every side, and I am
afflicted and despised by my *enemies*. They scorn my *soul*, and
revile it as an *evildoer* who has broken faith with them; they
deride my walking towards you and consider it *foolish*. They
think I am *senseless* because I walk in this *narrow* and *thorny
path* and do not go along with them in their *hypocritical broad way*.
  O LORD *Jesus Christ*; I fly to you under the *cross*; O dear
*Immanuel*, receive me, and carry me into *yourself*, through the
path of your *pilgrimage* in which you walked in *this world*;
namely, through your *anguish, passion*, and *death*. Make me
conformable to *your example*; send your good *angel* along with me
to show me the *way* through the horrible, thorny wilderness of
*this world*. Assist me in my *misery*; comfort me with that
comfort with which the *angel* comforted you in the garden, when
you prayed to your Father and sweated great *drops of blood*.
Support me in my *anguish* and *persecution*, under the
*reproach* of the *devils* and all wicked men who do not know you.

<summary>The author concludes a prayer for the soul to become a vessel for God's harmony and Zion, then begins a new prayer seeking Christ's protection and companionship while facing the world's persecution.</summary>

> and refuse to walk in your paths. O great *Love* of GOD, they do not know your way, and do this in blindness, through the *deceit* of the *devil*. Have pity on them, and bring them out of their *darkness* into your *light*, so that they may learn to *know* themselves, and how they lie captive in the *filth* and *mire* of the *devil*, in a dark *dungeon* fast bound with *three chains*. O great GOD, have mercy upon *Adam* and his children, redeem them in *Christ the new Adam.*

> I fly to you, O *Christ*, GOD and man, in this pilgrimage and journey which I must take through this *dark valley*, despised and troubled on all sides, and accounted an ungodly, wicked man. O LORD, it is your *judgment* upon me; so that my *sins* and *inbred corruption* may be *judged* in this earthly pilgrimage before you, and I, as a *curse*, be made an open spectacle on which your *anger* may satisfy itself, and thereby may take the *eternal reproach* away from me. It is the token of your *love*; by which you bring me into the *reproach, anguish, suffering* and *death* of my *Saviour Jesus Christ*, so that I may die from *vanity* and spring up in *His Spirit* with a *new life*, through His *reproach, ignominy*, and *death*.

> I beseech you, O *Christ*, you *patient Lamb* of GOD, grant me patience in this my *way of the cross*, through all *your anguish* and *reproach, your death* and *passion, your scorn* and *contempt* upon the *cross*, where you were despised in my place; and bring me in it, as a *patient lamb* to you, into your victory. Let me live with you in *you*: and you, convert my persecutors, who (unknown to themselves) by their reproaching sacrifice my *vanity* and *inbred sins* before your *anger*. They do not know what they do; they think they do me harm, but they do me good! They do that for me which I should do myself before you. For I should daily lay open and acknowledge my *shame* and *vileness* before you, and thereby *sink* myself down into the *death* of your *beloved Son*, so that my *shame* might die in his *death*. But I being too negligent, weary, faint, and feeble, you therefore use these my *enemies* in your *anger*, to open and discover my *vileness* before you, which your *wrath* takes hold of, and *sinks* it down into the *death of my Saviour*.

> O merciful GOD, my vain *flesh* cannot know how well you intend toward me, when you allow my *enemies* to take my *vileness* from me, and sacrifice it before you. My *earthly mind* supposes that you afflict me for my *sins*, and I am extremely perplexed at it; but *your Spirit*, in my *inward new man*, tells me that it is of your *love* toward me, and that you intend good to me by it. When you allow my *enemies* to persecute me, it is best for me that they perform the work in

But first bring them to the *light*, so that they may *know* you and give you thanks.
> O *merciful* GOD in *Christ Jesus*, I beseech you, out of your deep *love* toward us poor men, which you have manifested in me, in the *hidden man*, call us all in you, to you. Oh, stir yourself in us yet once again in this last trouble; as your *anger* is kindled in us, do resist it, lest it swallow us up completely, both *soul* and *body*.
> O you *dawning of the day-spring* of GOD, break forth to the *full!* Are you not already risen? Manifest your *holy city Zion*, your *holy Jerusalem*, in us. O great GOD! I see you in the depth of your *power* and *strength*. Awaken me completely in you, so that I may be made alive in you. Break off the *tree* of your *anger* in us, and let your *love* spring forth and bud in us. O LORD, I lie down in your sight, and beseech you not to rebuke us in your *anger*. Are we not your *possession* which you have purchased? *Forgive* all our *sins*, and deliver us from the evil of your *wrath*, and from the malice and envy of the *devil*; and bring us under your *cross* in patience into *paradise* again. *Amen.*

*Here follows a prayer or dialogue between the poor soul and the noble Virgin Sophia, in the inward ground of man—namely, between the Spirit of Christ in the new birth, out of His humanity in us, and the soul. Showing how great a joy there is in the heaven of the newly-regenerate man; and how lovingly and graciously the noble Sophia presents herself to her bridegroom the soul, when it enters into repentance, and how the soul behaves toward her when she appears to it.*

# The Gates of the Paradisiacal Garden of Roses.

->*This is understood by none but the Children of Christ, who have known it by Experience.*<-

WHEN Christ the *Corner-stone* stirs Himself in the extinguished *Image* of Man, in his sincere *Conversion* and *Repentance*; then *Virgin Sophia* appears in the stirring of the *Spirit of Christ*, in the extinguished *Image*, in her *virgin’s attire* before the soul. At this, the soul is so amazed and astonished in its *uncleanness*, that all its *sins* immediately awake in it, and it *trembles* before her. For then the *judgment* passes upon the *sins* of the soul, so that it even draws back in its unworthiness, being *ashamed* in the presence of its *fair Love*, and enters into *itself*, feeling and acknowledging itself utterly unworthy to receive such a *jewel*. This is understood by those who are of our *tribe*, and have tasted of this *heavenly gift*, and by none else. But the *noble Sophia* draws near in the *essence* of the soul, and *kisses* it in a friendly manner, and *tinctures* its *dark fire* with her *rays of love*, and shines through it with her bright and powerful *influence*. Penetrated with the strong sense and feeling of this, the soul skips in its body for great *joy*, and in the strength of this *virgin love*, exults and praises the great GOD for his blessed gift of *grace*.

I will set down here a short description of how it is when the *bride* thus embraces the *bridegroom*, for the consideration of the reader, who *perhaps* has not yet been in this *wedding-chamber*. It may be that he will be desirous to follow us, and to enter into the *inner-choir*, where the soul joins hands and dances with *Sophia* or the *Divine Wisdom*.

->*When that which is mentioned above comes to pass, the soul rejoices in its body, and says,*<-

PRAISE, thanksgiving, strength, honor, and glory be to you, O great GOD, in your *power* and *sweetness*, because you have redeemed me from the *anguish* of the *fiery driver*. O you *fair Love*! My heart embraces you; *where have you been so long? It seemed to me I was in Hell* in the *anger* of GOD. O gracious *Love*! abide with me, I beseech you, and be my joy and comfort. Lead me in the right way.

<summary>This page describes the mystical encounter between the soul and Divine Wisdom (Sophia) during true repentance, where the soul’s "dark fire" is transformed by divine love. It introduces a dialogue of praise and joy as the soul celebrates its redemption from spiritual anguish.</summary>
<keywords>Sophia, Divine Wisdom, Corner-stone, conversion, repentance, mystical union, dark fire, rays of love, fiery driver, spiritual joy</keywords>

I give myself up into your *Love*. I am *dark* before you, do enlighten me. O noble *Love*, give me your sweet *Pearl*; put it I pray you into me.
O great GOD in *Christ Jesus*, I praise and magnify you now in your Truth, in your great Power and Glory, because you have forgiven me my *Sins*, and filled me with your *Strength*. I shout for *Joy* before you in my *new Life*, and extol you in your *Firmament* of Heaven, which none can open but *your Spirit* in your Mercy. My Bones rejoice in your *Strength*, and my Heart delights itself in your *Love*. Thanks be to you for ever, because you have delivered me out of *Hell*, and turned *Death* into *Life* in me. O sweet *Love*! Let me not depart from you again. Grant me your *Garland* of *Pearl*, and abide in me. O be my own proper Possession, that I may rejoice in you for ever.

---

->*Upon this, Virgin Sophia says to the Soul,*<-

**M**Y noble *Bridegroom*, my *Strength* and Power, you are a thousand Times welcome. Why have you forgotten me so long, that I have been forced in great *Grief* to stand *without the Door and knock*? Have I not always called you and entreated you? But you have turned away your Countenance from me, and your Ears have declined my Entreaties. You could not see my *Light*, for you did walk in the *Valley of Darkness*. I was *very near* you, and entreated you continually, but your *Sinfulness* held you Captive in *Death*, so that you knew me not. I came to you in great *Humility* and called you, but you were *rich* in the Power of the *Anger* of GOD, and did not regard my *Humility* and *Lowliness*. You had taken the *Devil* to be your *Paramour*, who has defiled you thus, and built up his *Fort of Prey* in you, and turned you quite away from my *Love* and *Faith* into his *Hypocritical Kingdom of Falsehood*; wherein you have committed much *Sin* and *Wickedness*, and torn your *Will* off from my *Love*. You have broken the *Bond of Wedlock*, and set your *Love* and *Affection* upon a *Stranger*, and suffered me your *Bride*, whom GOD did give you, to stand alone in the *extinguished Substance*, without the *Power* of your fiery *Strength*. I could not be joyful without your fiery *Strength*, for you are my *Husband*; my shining *Brightness* is made *manifest* by you. You can *manifest* my hidden *Wonders* in your fiery *Life*, and bring them into *Majesty*; and yet without me you are but a *dark House*, wherein is nothing but *Anguish*, *Misery*, and horrible *Torment*.
O noble *Bridegroom*, stand still with your Countenance towards me, and give me your Rays of Fire. Bring your Desire into me,

O my *bridegroom*, how well I am now that I am in *union* with you! ->*The Soul speaks again to its noble Sophia, its Love, who is born again in it.*<-

O my noble *pearl* and opened *flame* of *light* in my anxious *fiery life*, how you *change* me into your *joy*! O beautiful *love*, I have broken my *faith* with you in my father *Adam*, and with my *fiery strength* I have turned myself to the *pleasure* and *vanity* of the *outward world*. I had fallen in *love* with a *stranger*, and would have been forced to walk in the *valley* of *darkness* in this *strange love*, if you had not come into the *house* of my *misery* in your great *faithfulness*—by your piercing through and destroying GOD’S *anger*, *hell*, and dark *death*, and restoring your *meekness* and *love* to my *fiery life*.
O sweet *love*; you have brought the *water* of eternal *life* out of the *fountain* of GOD with you into me, and refreshed me in my great *thirst*. I behold in you the *mercy* of GOD, which was hidden from me before by that *strange love*. In you I can *rejoice*; you change my *anguish* of *fire* into great *joy* within me. O lovely *love*, give me your *pearl*, so that I may continue in this *joy* forever.

->*Upon this, the noble Sophia answers the soul again and says,*<-

My dear *love* and faithful *treasure*, you greatly rejoice me in your *beginning*. I have indeed broken into you through the deep *gates* of GOD, through GOD’S *anger*, through *hell* and *death*, into the house of your *misery*; and I have graciously bestowed my *love* upon you and delivered you from the *chains* and *bonds* with which you were fast bound. I have kept my *faith* with you, even though you have not kept yours

with me. But you desire an exceedingly great thing from me now, which I cannot willingly trust in your hands. You would have my *Pearl* as your *own property*. Remember, I pray, O my beloved *Bridegroom*, that you carelessly lost it before in *Adam*; and you yourself still stand in great danger and walk in *two* dangerous kingdoms. For in your *Original Fire*, you walk in that country where GOD calls himself a *strong, jealous God* and a *consuming Fire*. The other kingdom you walk in is the *outward world*, where you dwell in the vain, corrupt *flesh* and *blood*, and where the *pleasures* of the *world* and the *assaults* of the *devil* beset you every hour. Perhaps in your great *joy*, you might bring *earthliness* back into my beauty, and thereby *darken* my *pearl*. Or you might possibly grow *proud*, as *Lucifer* did when he had the *pearl* in his possession, and so turn away from the *harmony* of GOD as he did; and then I would be deprived of *my love* forever after.

No. I will keep my *Pearl* within myself and dwell in the *heaven* within you—in your *extinguished* humanity that is now *revived* in me. I will reserve my *Pearl* for *Paradise* until you put away this *earthliness* from you, and then I will give it to you to possess. But I will readily present to you my *pleasant countenance* and the *sweet rays* of the *Pearl* during the time of this *earthly life*. I will dwell with the *Pearl* itself in the *inner choir* and be your faithful, loving *bride*. I cannot wed myself to your *earthly flesh*, for I am a *heavenly queen*, and *my kingdom is not of this world*. Yet I will not cast your *outward life* away, but refresh it often with my *rays of love*; for your *outward humanity* shall return again. But I cannot admit the *beast of vanity* to my embraces; neither did GOD create it in *Adam* with the purpose for it to be so *coarse* and earthly. But in *Adam*, your *desire*—through the power of its strong lust—formed this *bestial coarseness* from and with all the *essences* of the *awakened vanity* of the *earthly property*, in which *heat* and *cold*, *pain* and *enmity*, *division* and *corruption* exist.

Now, my dear *love* and *bridegroom*, simply yield yourself up into *my will*; I will not forsake you in your danger during this earthly life. Though the *anger* of GOD should pass over you, so that you grow frightened and disheartened, or think that I have *deserted* you, yet I will be with you and preserve you; for you yourself do not know what your task is. You must *work* and *bear fruit* during this lifetime. You are the *root* of this *pearl-tree*; *branches* must be produced from you, which must all be brought forth in *anguish*. But I come forth together with your *branches* in their *sap*, and produce *fruit* upon your

## An Evening Prayer,

->*When we have finished our daily employment and are going to rest.*<-

> I lift my *heart* to you, O GOD, the *fountain* of *eternal life*, and give you thanks through *Jesus Christ* your beloved son, our *Lord* and *Savior*, for having protected and preserved me this day from all *harm* that might have befallen me. I commend to your disposal my *condition* and *employment*, together with the *work* of my hands, and humbly rest them upon you. Fill my soul with your Spirit so that neither that *great enemy* the *devil*, nor any other *evil influence* or *desire*, may find a *harbor* within it. Let my mind only *delight* in you in your *temple*, and let your *good angel* stay with me, so that I may *rest* safely in your *power*, and under your *protection*. *Amen.*
Revelation 21:6. "I am Alpha and Omega, the beginning and the end. I will give to him who is thirsty from the fountain of the water of life freely. He who overcomes shall inherit all things, and I will be his God, and he shall be my son."

### THE
# SECOND BOOK.
### OF
## TRUE RESIGNATION,
### SHOWING

*How* man must *daily die* to his own *will* in *self*; *how* he must bring his *desire* into **GOD**, and *what* he should *ask* and *desire* of **GOD**. Likewise *how* he must *spring up* out of the *dying sinful man*, with a *new mind* and *will* through the *Spirit of Christ*.

### ALSO,

*What* the *Old* and *New Man* are, and *what* each of them is in *life, will,* and *practice*.

---

->Written in the *German language*,<-
->(In the year 1622)<-
->By **JACOB BOEHME**.<-

Send your *holy angel* along with me, to turn the temptations of the *devil* and *corrupt nature* away from me. Preserve me from the malice of *evil men*, make all my *enemies* reconcilable to me, and bring my *mind* into your *vineyard*, so that I may labor in my office and employment, and behave as your obedient servant in it. Bless me, and all that I am to go about and do this day, with the blessing of your *love* and *mercy*. Continue your *grace* and *love* in *Jesus Christ* toward me, and give me a mind *cheerfully* to follow your guidance and carry out your appointment. Bless me, and all that I am to go about and do this day, with the blessing of your *love* and *mercy*. Continue your *grace* and *love* in *Jesus Christ* toward me, and give me a mind to *cheerfully* follow your guidance and carry out your appointment. Let *your Holy Spirit* guide me in my *beginning* and my *progress*, on to my final end, and be the *willing, working,* and *accomplishing* of everything in me. *Amen.*

---

<meta>This page begins Chapter I of the second book, "Of True Resignation," which follows the prayers and scriptural citations of the previous section. It introduces the spiritual fall of Lucifer and Adam as the primary examples of the danger of self-will.</meta>

# OF TRUE RESIGNATION

## Chapter I.

We have a clear example in *Lucifer*, and also in *Adam* the first man, of what *Self* does when it gets the *Light of Nature* to be *its own*, and may walk with the *Understanding* in *its own dominion*. We see also in men learned in arts and sciences, that when *they* get the *Light* of this outward world or *Nature* into the possession of their *Reason*, nothing comes of it but *pride of themselves*. And yet all the world so vehemently desires and seeks after this *Light* as the best *treasure*; and indeed it is the *best treasure* this *world* offers, if it be rightly used.

2. But while *Self*, namely *Reason*, is captivated and fast bound in a close and strong prison—that is to say, in the *Anger* of GOD and in *earthliness*—it is very dangerous for a man to make use of the *Light of Knowledge* in *Self*, as if it were in the possession of *Self*.

3. For the *Wrath* of the eternal and temporal nature will soon take pleasure in it, and then *Self* and a man's own *reason* will rise up in *pride* and depart from the true resigned *humility* toward GOD, and will no longer eat of the *Fruit of Paradise*, but of the *property of self*, namely, of that dominion of life where *good* and *evil* are mixed, as *Lucifer* and *Adam* did. They both entered with the *desire of self* back again into the *original* out of which the *creatures* were brought forth, and into the *condition* of the *creatures*: *Lucifer* into the *center* and *wrathful nature*, into the *matrix* or womb which brings forth *fire*; and *Adam* into the *earthly nature*, into the *matrix* of the outward world, namely, into the *lust* after *good* and *evil*.

4. This happened to them both because they had the *light of understanding* shining in *Self*, in which they could behold *themselves*, whereby the *spirit of self* went into the *imagination* (namely, into a *desire* to get to the *center*), so that they might exalt themselves in *might*, *power*, and *knowledge*. Now when *Lucifer* sought after the *mother of fire* in his *center* and thought to reign with it over the *love of God* and all the *angels*; and when *Adam* also desired to test in the *essence* what the *mother* or *root* was from which *evil* and *good* sprang, and purposely brought his *desire* into it in order to become *knowledgeable* and full of *understanding* by it: both *Lucifer* and *Adam* were captivated in their evil or false *desire* in the *mother*, and broke themselves off from *resignation* which proceeds from GOD, and so were caught

<summary>This chapter uses the examples of Lucifer and Adam to illustrate how the pursuit of knowledge through self-will and reason leads to pride and a departure from God. It warns that when the light of understanding is used for self-exaltation rather than in humble resignation, it leads to spiritual captivity.</summary>

by the *spirit* of the *will*, through the *desire* in the *Mother*. This *desire* immediately gained *dominion* in *nature*; and so *Lucifer* stuck fast in the *wrathful source* of *fire*. That *fire* became manifest in the *spirit* of his *will*, by which the *creature* in its *desire* became an *enemy* to the *love* and *meekness* of God.

5. *Adam*, in like manner, was immediately caught by the earthly *Mother*, which is *evil* and *good*, created out of the *love* and *anger* of God and compacted into one *substance*. Thereupon, the earthly *property* instantly gained *dominion* in *Adam*; from that point, *heat* and *cold*, *envy* and *anger*, and all *malice* and *opposition* to God became manifest and ruled within him.

6. But if they had not brought the *light* of *knowledge* into *self*, then the *mirror* <note>glass</note> of the *knowledge* of the *center* and of the *origin* of the *creature*—namely, of the *power* which it had in *itself*—would not have been manifested, from which *imagination* and *lust* arose.

7. We also often see today how the same *error* brings danger upon the *enlightened children of God*. In them, when the *sun* of the great presence of God's *holiness shines*, through which *life* passes into *triumph*, *reason* then beholds itself there as in a *mirror* <note>glass</note>. Then the *will* continues in *self*, in *its own* searching, and tries to discover what the *center* is from which the *light* shines, and by *its own* motion and strength forces itself into it. From this arise abominable *pride* and *self-love*, so that the creature's own *reason*—which is only a *mirror* or *glass* of the eternal *wisdom*—imagines itself to be greater than it is. Then, whatever it does, it thinks God's *will* acts *in* and *through* it, and that it is a *prophet*. Yet it is moved only by *itself* and continues in *its own desire*, in which the *center* of nature immediately rises up and enters into that *false desire* of *self* against God; thus the *will* enters into *self-conceit* and *exaltation*.

8. Then the subtle *devil* insinuates himself into the *creature* and sifts the *center* of nature, bringing *evil* or *false desires* into it, so that a man becomes, as it were, *drunk* with *self*. He still persuades himself that he is driven by God; by this means the *good beginning*, in which the *divine light* shone in *nature*, becomes spoiled, and so the *light* of God departs from him.

9. Yet the *outward light* of the *outward nature* still remains shining in the *creature*. For its own *self* throws itself into it and supposes that it is the *first light of God*, but it is not so. And into this *self-exaltation* in the *light* of its *outward reason*, the *devil* throws himself again (though in the *first light*, which was *divine*, he was forced to depart), returning with

10. This house that is thus *swept and garnished* is the *light of reason* in *self*. For if a man brings his *desire* and *will* into God, and goes on in abstinence from *this wicked life*, and heartily desires the *love of God*, then *that love will manifest itself* to him with its most friendly and cheerful countenance, by which the *outward light* also is kindled. For where the *light of God* is kindled, there *all* will be *light*; the *devil* cannot stay there but must depart from there. And then he searches through the *Mother of the Original of Life, namely*, the *Centre*, but it has become a *dry, feeble place*. For the *anger of God, namely*, the *Centre of Nature*, is in *its own property* altogether *feeble, barren, and dry*, and cannot gain the *dominion in its own wrathful principle*. *Satan* searches through these places to find an *open gate* to enter with his *desire*, and so to sift the soul so that it might come to *exalt itself*.

11. And now if the *spirit* of the *will* of the *creature* throws itself with the *light of reason* into the *Centre, namely*, into *self*, and enters into *self-exaltation*, then it goes forth again from the *light of God*. Presently the *devil* finds an *open gate* for him to enter in at, and a *garnished house* to dwell in, *namely*, the *light of reason*. Then he takes to himself the *seven forms* of the *property of life* in *self, namely*, the *flatterers* which have departed from God into *self*: and there he enters and puts his *desire* into the *lust of self* and *evil imaginations*, wherein the *spirit* of the *will* beholds itself in the *forms of the properties of life* in the *outward light*. Then the man sinks into *himself* as if he were drunk, and the *stars* <note>In Boehme's cosmology, the "stars" often represent the astral influences and the natural, outward reason of the world.</note> lay hold on him and bring their strong influences into him (*into outward reason*) so that he might seek the *wonders of God there*, so that they may manifest themselves within it. For *all creatures groan and long after God*, and though the *stars* cannot apprehend the *spirit of God*, yet they would rather have a *house of light* wherein they may rejoice, than a *house shut up* wherein they can have no *rest*.

The deluded man then supposes that he has now reached the *goal*, and that *honor* is due to him because he has gained the *Understanding* of *Reason*. He never considers that the *Devil* makes himself merry with *his desire* in his *seven forms of life* of the *Center of Nature*, nor what abominable error he establishes.

13. From *this understanding of reason*, false *Babel* is brought forth in the *Christian Church* on Earth, where men rule and teach by the *conclusions of reason*. They have set the *child*—who is drunk on its own pride and *self-desire*—as a *fair virgin* upon the throne.

14. But the *Devil* has entered into its *seven forms of life* of the *Center*, *namely*, into its own *self-conceited reason*, and continually brings *his desire* into this adorned virgin, which the *stars* receive. He is her *beast* on which she *rides*, well-adorned with her own powers of life, as may be seen in the *Revelation* of *St. John*. Thus this *Child of Self* has taken into its possession the *outward appearance of Divine Holiness*, *namely*, the *Light of Reason*, and supposes itself to be the *fair child* in the house, even though the *Devil* has his lodging within it all the while.

15. And so it is with all those who have been *once enlightened* by God, and afterwards turn away again from *true resignation*, and wean themselves from the pure milk of their mother, *namely*, true *humility*.

# Chapter II.

HERE *Reason* will object and say,
> "Is it not right for a
> man to attain the Light of God, and also the light
> of outward nature and reason, so that he may be
> able to order his life wisely, as the Scripture
> directs?"

2. Yes, it is *very right*; nothing can be more profitable to a man, nor is he capable of anything better; indeed, it is a *treasure* above all *earthly treasures* for a man to have the *Light of God* and of *time*, for it is the *eye of time* and of *eternity*.

3. But notice how you ought to use it: when the *Light of God first* manifests itself in the soul, it shines forth as light

from a candle, and *kindles the outward light of reason* immediately; yet it does not yield itself *wholly* up to *reason*, so as to be under the *dominion of the outward man*. No, *the outward man* beholds *himself in this through-shining luster*, as he does his likeness in a looking-glass; by which he presently learns to *know himself*; which is good and profitable for him.

4. Now when he does so, *reason*, which is the *creaturely self*, can do no better than to behold itself in the *self of the creature*, and not enter with the *will* of the *desire* into the *center* to seek *itself*. If it does, it breaks itself off from the *substance* of GOD (which rises together with the *light* of GOD, of which the soul ought to eat and *refresh itself* with), and *eats* of the *outward substance and light*, and thereby draws the *venom* into itself again.

5. ¶ The *will* of the creature ought to *sink wholly into itself* with *all its reason and desire*, accounting itself an unworthy child that is not at all worthy of such high *grace*; nor should it *arrogate* any knowledge or understanding to *itself*, or desire of GOD to have any *understanding* in its *creaturely self*. Instead, it should sincerely and simply *sink* down into the *grace* and *love* of GOD in *Christ Jesus*, and desire to be, as it were, dead to itself and *its own reason* in the *divine life*, and wholly resign itself to the *Spirit* of GOD in *love*, so that He may do how and what He will with it, as with His own instrument.

6. Its own *reason* ought not to enter into any *speculation on divine things* or into the *ground of human matters*; nor should it *will* and *desire* anything but the *grace* of GOD in *Christ*. And as a child *continually* longs for the breasts of the mother, so must its *hunger* be *continually* entering into the *love* of GOD, and not suffer itself to be broken off from that *hunger* by any means. When the *outward reason* triumphs in the *light*, saying, "*I have the true child*," then the *will* of the *desire* must *bow* itself down to the earth and bring itself into the deepest *humility* and *most simple ignorance*, and say, "You are foolish, and have nothing but the grace of God." You must wrap yourself up in *that belief* with great *humility*, and become *nothing* at all in *yourself*, and neither know nor love *yourself*. All that you have, or that is in you, must *esteem* itself as *nothing* but a *mere instrument* of GOD; and you must bring your *desire* only into GOD’s *mercy*, and go forth from all *your own* knowing and willing; and esteem it as nothing at all, nor ever entertain any *will* to enter into it again.

7. As soon as this is done, the *natural will* becomes *weak and faint*, and then the *Devil* is not able to *sift* it any longer with his *evil desire*, for the places of his *rest* become very *power-*

Whereupon the *humbled soul* presently desires to *sink down into that light*, and esteems itself to be *nothing*, and quite unworthy of it.

9. When the *Spirit of God rises up* as a *fire* and *flame* of *love*, then the *spirit of the soul descends*, and says,

In such an humble and total *resignation* the *spark of divine power* falls into the *center* of the *forms of life*, like a spark into tinder, and *kindles it*, namely, the *fire of the soul*, which *Adam* made to be a *dark coal* in himself, so that it *glimmers*. And when the *light* of *divine power* has kindled itself within it, the creature must go on as an *instrument* of GOD’S *Spirit*, and speak what the *Spirit* of GOD dictates to it; and then it is no longer in its *own proper possession*, but is the *instrument* of GOD.

11. But the *will* of the soul must *without ceasing*, in this fiery-driving, *sink into nothing*, namely, into the *deepest humility* in the sight of GOD. For no sooner does the *will* of the soul *in the least measure* go on in its *own speculation* or *searching*, but *Lucifer* lays hold of it in the *center* of the *forms of life*, and sifts it, so that it enters into *self*. It must therefore *continue close* to *resigned humility*, as a well does to its spring, and must suck and drink of GOD’S *fountain*, and not depart from the ways of GOD at all.

12. For as soon as the soul *eats of self*, and of the *light of outward reason*, it goes on in its *own opinion*; and then its doings, which it sets forth as *divine*, are but from the *outward constellation*, or *influence of the stars*, which presently lays hold on the soul, and makes it *dry*. And then the soul goes on in *errors*, until it yields itself up again into *resignation*, and

13. This may be seen clearly in the *saints* of GOD from the beginning of the world. For many who have been driven by the *Spirit* of GOD have yet often departed from *resignation* into *self*—namely, into *their own reason and will*—in which *Satan* has cast them into *sins* and into the *anger* of GOD; as appears by *David* and *Solomon*, and also by the *patriarchs, prophets*, and *apostles*, who often committed *great errors* when they departed from *resignation* into *self*—namely, into *their own reason and lust*.

14. Therefore, it is necessary for the *children of God* to *know* how to behave themselves when they wish to *learn* the way of God. They must beat down and cast away their very *thoughts* and *desire* nothing, nor have the *least will* to *learn* anything, unless they find themselves to be in *true resignation*; so that *God's Spirit* leads, teaches, and guides *man's spirit*, and so that the human *will* which is attached to itself is *wholly* broken off from *its own lust* and resigned to GOD.

15. All *speculation* regarding the *wonders* of GOD is very dangerous, for the *spirit* of the *will* may soon be *captured* by it, unless the *spirit* of the *will* follows or walks after the *Spirit of God*; then it has power in *resigned humility* to behold the *wonders* of GOD.

16. I do not say that a man should search and learn nothing in *natural arts and sciences*; no, such knowledge is useful to him. But a man must not *begin* with *his own reason*. Man ought not only to govern his life by the *light of outward reason*, which is good in itself, but should sink with *that light* into the *deepest humility* before GOD, and set the *spirit* and *will* of GOD *foremost* in all his searching, so that the *light of reason* may see and *know* things through the *light* of GOD. And though *reason* may be very wise in its own sphere and help a man to *much knowledge*, yet it must not claim such *wisdom* and *knowledge* for *itself* as if they were in its own possession, but give the glory for it to GOD, to whom alone *all wisdom and knowledge belong*.

17. For the *more deeply reason* sinks itself down into *simple humility* in the *sight* of GOD, and the *more unworthy* it accounts itself in His sight, the *more truly* it dies to *self-desire*, and the *more thoroughly* the *Spirit of God* penetrates it and brings it into the *highest knowledge*, so that at length it may come to behold the *great mysteries* and *wonders* of GOD. For the *Spirit of God* works *only* in resigned humility, in that which neither

18. For GOD has not created us for *ourselves* only, but to be *Instruments of His Wonders*, by which He desires to *manifest His Wonders*. The *resigned Will* trusts GOD and expects all good from Him alone; but *Self-Will* rules itself, for it is broken off from GOD. *All that Self-Will does is Sin* and against GOD; for it has gone out of that Order in which He created it, into *Disobedience*, and desires to be its *own Lord and Master*.

19. When a man’s *own Will* dies to *itself*, then it is free from *Sin*, for it *desires* nothing but what GOD *desires* for His creature; it *desires* only to do that for which GOD has created it, and that which GOD will do through it; and though it is and must be the *Doing*, yet it is but the *Instrument of the Doing* by which GOD does what He wills.

20. For this is the *true Faith* in man, *namely*, to *die to himself*; that is, from *his own Desire*; and in all his undertakings and designs to bring his *Desire* into the *Will of GOD*, and *claim* the doing of nothing for *himself*, but esteem himself in *all* his doings to be but a *Servant* or *Minister of GOD*, and to think that *all he does and undertakes is for GOD*. For in such a disposition the *Spirit of God* leads him into true *Uprightness* and *Faithfulness* toward his *Neighbor*. For he thinks thus to himself,
> 'I do my work not for myself, but for God, who has called
> 'and appointed me to do it; I am but a servant in His vineyard.'
He listens continually for the *Voice* of his *Master*, who *within* him commands him what he shall do. The *Lord* speaks in him and bids him do what He would have done by him.

21. But *Self* does what *outward Reason from the Stars* commands, into which *Reason* the *Devil* flies with his *Desire*. *Whatever Self does is without the Will of God*, and is done altogether in the *Phantasy*, so that the *Anger of GOD* may accomplish its *Pastime* with it.

22. *No work done without the Will of God can reach the Kingdom of God*; it is all but an *unprofitable Imagery, or self-wrought Work*, in this great *Agitation of Mankind*. For *Nothing* is pleasing to God but what He Himself does through the *Will* as His *Instrument*. For there is but *One only God in the Essence of all Essences*, and all *That* which works with Him in that *Essence* is *one Spirit with Him*; but that which works in *itself*, in its *own Will*, is in *itself* only and not in *His Dominion*. It is indeed under that *universal Dominion of Nature* by which He holds

> "Every plant which my Heavenly Father has 
> "not planted shall be rooted out and burned in the fire."

All the *works* of man which he has performed *without* the *Will* of GOD shall be *burned up* in the *final fire* and given to the *Wrath* of GOD—that is, to the *Pit of Darkness*—to occupy itself with. For *Christ* says,
> "He who is not with me is against me, and he who 
> "does not gather with me scatters."

Whoever *works* and does not do it in a *resigned will* with confidence in GOD, only makes things desolate and *scatters*; it is not acceptable to GOD. For nothing is *pleasing* to Him except that which He wills with His *Spirit* and does through *His own instrument*.

24. Therefore, whatever is done by the conclusions of *human self* in matters of *religion* is a *mere fiction*. It is *Babel*, and only a *work of the stars* and of the *outward world*, and not acknowledged by GOD to be *His work*. It is only the *play* of the *wrestling wheel of nature*, in which *good* and *evil* wrestle with each other; what one *builds*, the other *destroys*. And this is the great misery of the *vain turmoils* of men, the outcome of which must be left to the *Judgment of GOD*.

25. Therefore, whoever *stirs* or *labors much* in *such turmoil* *works* only for the *judgment of GOD*; for not one bit of it is *perfect* and *permanent*. It must all be separated in the *putrefaction*. For that which is performed in the *anger of GOD* will be *received* by it and kept in the *mystery of its desire* until the day of GOD’S *judgment*, when *evil* and *good* shall be *severed*.

26. But if a man turns and goes out from *himself*, and enters into the *Will of God*, then the *good* which has been performed in and by him shall also be freed from the *evil* that worked. As *Isaiah* says,
> "though your sins be as red as scarlet, yet if you 
> "turn and repent, they shall become as wool, yes, as white as 
> "snow."

For the *evil* shall be swallowed up in the *wrath* of GOD into *death*, and the *good* shall spring forth, like a *sprout*, out of the wild *earth*.

<summary>This page explains that human religious efforts created outside of God's will are temporary "fictions" compared to those done through divine resignation. It warns that only works planted by God survive the final judgment, while self-driven works are consumed by wrath.</summary>
<keywords>resignation, Will of God, Babel, judgment, Christ, Isaiah, self-will, wrestling wheel of nature, putrefaction</keywords>

# Chapter III.

WHOEVER therefore intends to do any *good* and *perfect Work*, in which he hopes eternally to rejoice, let him *depart from himself*, namely, *from his own Desire* and enter into *Resignation*, into the *Will* of GOD, and *work* with GOD. And then though the *earthly Desire* of *Self* in Flesh and Blood cleaves to him, yet if the *Will* of the *Soul* does not *receive* that *Desire* into it, *Self* cannot perform any *Work*. For the *resigned Will* continually *destroys* the *Substance of Self* again, so that the *Anger* of GOD cannot reach it. And if it should happen to reach it *sometimes*, as may be the case, yet the *resigned Will prevails* with its superior Power; and then it bears the *Figure* of a *Victorious Work* in the *Wonders*, and may inherit the *Filiation* or *Childship*. Therefore it is not good to *speak* or *do* any thing, when *Reason* is kindled in and by the *Desire* of *Self*. Because that *Desire* springs from, and *works* in, the *Anger* of GOD; by which a *man* would suffer *loss*. For his *work* is brought into that *anger* and kept there until the *Great Day of* GOD'S *Judgment*.

2. Every evil or false *Desire*, by which a man *devises how* to gather to himself by craft much worldly gain from his neighbor to his neighbor's hurt, is taken into the *Anger* of GOD and belongs to the *Judgment*. In which *all things shall be made manifest*, and every *Power* and *Essence*, every *Cause* and *Effect*, both in *good* and *evil*, shall be presented to everyone in the *Mystery of the Revelation*.

3. All evil *works done purposely* belong to the *Judgment of God*. But he who *turns* from the *Will goes out from the Power* of them, and those his *works* belong to the *fire*. *All things shall and must be made manifest in the end*. For therefore GOD brought His *working Power* into *Essence* or *Substance*, that His *Love* and *Anger* might be *made manifest*, and become a representation of His *Deeds of Wonder*, to His *Glory*.

4. And *every creature* ought to know, that it should continue in *that condition* in which it was *created*; otherwise it runs on in *contrariety* and *enmity* to the *Will* of GOD, and brings itself into *pain*. For every *intelligent creature* that has lost its place or state in which GOD first created it, is in *disorder* and *misery*, until it *recovers* the same. A *creature* which is *created* out of *darkness* has no *pain* in the *darkness*; as a *venomous serpent* has no *pain* from its *venom*. The *venom* is its *life*; but if it should lose its *venom*, and have some *good thing* instead

<summary>The author explains that true, perfect work can only be achieved by surrendering self-will to the Will of God, which protects the soul from divine anger. Furthermore, all intentions and works will eventually be revealed in God's judgment, and creatures only find peace when remaining in their natural, created state.</summary>

brought into it, and be made manifest in its *Essence*, that would be *Pain* and *Death* to it. Thus *Good* is *Torment* to a Being whose Nature is *evil*, and *Evil* is in like Manner *Pain* and *Death* to the *good*.

5. Man was *created out of, for, and in Paradise*; *out of, for, and in the Love of* God; but if he brings himself into *Anger*, which is like a *poisonous Pain and Death*, then that contrary *Paradisaical Life of Love* is a *Pain and Torment* to him.

6. If the *Devil* had been created out of the *wrathful Matrix*, *for* and *in Hell*, and had not possessed the *Divine <term>Ens</term>* or *Essence*, he could have no *Pain* in *Hell*. But because he was *created for and in Heaven*, and yet stirred up the *Source* or *Property* of *Darkness* in himself, and thereby brought himself totally into *Darkness*, the *Light* is now a *Pain* to him; that is, it *causes* an *everlasting Despair of* GOD’S *Grace*, and a *continual Enmity toward* GOD, because GOD cannot endure him in *Himself*, but has cast him out. Therefore the *Devil* is angry and wrathful against *his own Mother*, from whose *Essence* and *Substance* he has his *Origin—namely*, the *eternal Nature*—which keeps him Prisoner in *his own Place*, as a rebel or fallen Spirit, and *sports* within him with its *Property of Anger*. And, seeing he would not take his part in promoting the *Divine Joy*, in and for which he was created, he must now do the opposite, and be an *Enemy* to all *Goodness*. For *all things are from God and in Him*; *Darkness* and *Light*, *Love* and *Anger*, *Fire* and *Light*; but he calls Himself *God* only regarding the *Light* of His *Love*.

7. There is an *eternal opposition* between *Darkness* and *Light*; neither of them *understands* the other, and neither of them *is* the other; and yet there is only *one Essence, Being, or Substance* in which they both exist. But there is a Difference in *Quality* and *Will*; yet the *Essence* or *Substance* is not *divided*, but a *Principle* makes the *Division*. So the one is as *nothing* within the *other*, and yet it *is* there, but not *manifested* in the *Property* of that Thing in which it resides.

8. For the *Devil* remained in his own Dominion or *Principality*—not indeed in that in which GOD *created* him, but in the *aching, painful Birth of Eternity*, in the *Center of Nature* and *Property of Wrath*, in the *Property* which produces *Darkness, Anguish* and *Pain*. Indeed he is a *Prince* in the *Place of this World*, but in the *first Principle*, in the *Kingdom of Darkness*, in the *Pit* or *Abyss*.

9. Not in the *Kingdom* of the *Sun, Stars*, and *Elements*; he is no *Lord* or *Prince* there, but in the *wrathful Part—namely*, in the *Root of the Evil* of every *Thing*; and yet he does not have Power to do whatever he pleases with *That*.

<summary>This page explores the ontological conflict between light and darkness, explaining that the Devil suffers because his heavenly origin conflicts with his chosen state of darkness. 10. For there is some *Good* in every *thing*, which holds the *Evil* captive and shut up in the *thing*; but it can walk and rule *only in the evil part* or *property* when it stirs up an *evil desire* in itself and brings its *desire* into *wickedness*. This indeed the *inanimate creatures* cannot do; but Man can do it through the *inanimate creature*, if he brings the *center* of his *will*, with the *desire from the eternal center, into it*, which is the *basis of enchantment* and *false magic*. The *will* of the *Devil* can also enter into that *evil* where man brings the *desire* of his soul, which is also born out of *Eternal Nature*.

11. For the *origin* of the soul and of *angels* out of the *Eternal Nature* is the same. But the *Devil* has no further power over *time*, or the *temporary condition* of this world, than in the *great Turba*, or *Turba Magna*, the *Curse*; wherever *that* kindles itself in the *eternal* and *temporal wrath*, there *he* is busy, as in *wars, fighting*, and *strife*, as also in *great storms without water*. In the *fire* he proceeds as far as the *Turba* (mischief or hurt) goes in great *showers* or *storms* of *thunder, lightning*, and *hail*; but he cannot *direct* them, for he is not *lord* or *master* over them, but *servant* only.

12. Thus the *creature* stirs up with its *desire* both *Good* and *Evil, Life* and *Death*. The *human* angelical *desire* stands in the *center* of the *eternal nature* which is without beginning; and whatever it *kindles* itself in, whether in *good* or *evil*, it accomplishes its *work in that*.

13. Now GOD *created* every *thing* for and *in that* where it should be—the *angels* for and in *Heaven*, and *Man* for and in *Paradise*. If, therefore, the *desire* of the *creature* goes out from *its own mother*, it then enters into the *contrary will* and into *enmity*, and it is tormented with the *contrariety* within it; thus a *false will* arises in a *good* one. Then the *good will* enters into its *nothingness* again—*namely*, into the *end of nature and creature*—and so leaves the *creature* in its own *evil* or *wickedness*, as appears by *Lucifer* and also by *Adam*. If the *will* of the *Love of GOD* had not met with *Adam* and, out of mere *mercy*, entered into *humanity* or *human nature* again, there could be no *good will* in *Man*.

14. Therefore all *speculation* and *inquiry* about *God's will* is a vain *thing* unless the mind is *converted*. For when the mind stands captive in the *self-desire of the earthly life*, it cannot comprehend what the *will* of GOD is; it runs only in *self*, from one *way* into another, and yet finds no *rest*; for *self-desire* evermore brings *disquiet*. But when it *sinks* itself wholly into the *mercy* of GOD, desiring to die *to itself*, and to have GOD'S *will* for a guide to the understanding, so

19. Therefore no man can make any excuse, as if he could not *will*. Indeed, while he remains stuck in *himself*, *in his own desire*, and *serves only the law of sin in the flesh*, he cannot. For he is held back as a *servant of sin*; but when he turns the *center* of his mind away and directs it into the *will* and *obedience of God*, then he can.

20. Now the *center* of the mind has come out of *eternity*, out of God’s *omnipotence*; it can bring itself into whatever it wills and wherever it wills. For *that which is out of the eternal has no law*. But the *will* has a *law* to obey God and is born out of the *mind*, and must not tear itself away from *that* out of which God created it.

21. Now God created the *will* of the *mind* for and in *paradise*, to be a companion with Him in the *kingdom of divine joy*. It ought not to have removed itself from there; but since it has removed itself from there, God has brought His will again into the *flesh*, and in His *newly brought-in will*, has given us *power* to bring our *wills* into it, and to kindle a *new light* within it, and so *become His children* again.

22. God *hardens no man*; but man’s own *will*, which goes on in the *fleshly life of sin*, *hardens his heart*. The *will of self* brings the *vanity* of this world into the *mind*, which is thereby shut up and continues to be so.

23. *God*, so far as He is called *God* and is *God*, cannot *will any evil*; for there is only *one will* in God, and that is *eternal love*, a *desire* for that which is like Him, *namely, power, beauty, and virtue*.

24. *God desires nothing* but *what is like His own desire*; His *desire* receives nothing but *what it is itself*.

25. God receives no *sinner* into His *power* and *virtue* unless the *sinner* goes out from his *sins* and enters with *desire* into God. And then, "He will not cast out those who so come to Him." He has given to the *will* an *open gate in Christ*, saying, "Come to me all you who are heavy laden with sins," and "I will refresh you; take my yoke upon you," that is, the *cross of the enmity in the flesh*. This was the yoke of *Christ*, which he had to bear for the *sins* of all men. This *cross* or *yoke* the *resigned will* must also take upon itself in the evil earthly sinful *flesh*, and bear it after *Christ* in *patience* and *hope of deliverance*. 28. Regard not any *scorn* of the *world*, considering that it only scorns your *enemy*, which has become a *fool* to it. No, you should yourself account it your *fool*, which *Adam* caused you to possess, and made to be your *false heir*. Cast out of the *house* the *son* of the *bondwoman*, that *strange child* which GOD did not give to be in the *house* of *life* in *Adam* at the beginning; for the *son* of the *bondwoman* must not inherit with the *son* of the *freewoman*.

29. The earthly will is but the *son* of the *bondwoman*. For the *four elements* should have been *man’s servants*, but *Adam* has brought them into the *sonship*, or *adopted* them into himself. Therefore GOD said to *Abraham*, when He had opened the *covenant* of the *promise* in him;

This *fountain* is the *renovation* of the *mind* or *will* of the *soul*.

30. 31. And though all the *devils* should press hard upon it and enter with their *desire* into the *fleshly mind*, yet the *will* of the soul must stand still and hide itself in the *death of Christ*, willing and desiring nothing but the *mercy of God*.

32. No *hypocritical flattery* or outward *comforting* of ourselves avails at all; such as when men will *cover sin* and iniquity in the flesh with the *satisfaction of Christ*, yet remain in the *self* still. *Christ* says, "Except you turn and become as children, you shall not see the kingdom of God." The *mind* must become as wholly *new* as in a *child* that knows nothing of *sin*. *Christ* also says, "You must be born anew, or else you shall not see the kingdom of God." There must arise a *will* that is wholly *new* in the *death of Christ*. It must be brought forth out of *Christ's incarnation* (or entering into humanity) and rise in *Christ's resurrection*.

33. Now before this can be done, the *will* of the soul must die in the *death of Christ*; for in *Adam* it received the *son of the bondwoman*—namely, *sin*—into it. The *will* must first cast this out, and the poor captive soul must wrap itself up in the *death of Christ* earnestly, with all the *power it has*, so that the *son of the bondwoman*—the *sin* that is in it—may die in the *death of Christ*.

34. In truth, *sin* must *die* in the *will* of the soul, or else there can be no *vision* of God. For the *earthly will*, in *sin* and the *wrathful nature*, shall not *see* God. It is only the *regenerated nature* that is capable of the *divine vision* or *enjoyment*. The soul *must* put on the *spirit and flesh of Christ*; it cannot inherit the kingdom of God in this *earthly tabernacle*. For the kingdom of *sin* clings to it outwardly, which must rot in the earth and rise again in *new power*.

35. Hypocrisy, flattery, and *verbal forgiveness* avail nothing. We must be *children*, not by *outward imputation*, but *by being born of God from within*, in the *new man*, who is resigned *in* and *to God*.

36. All such flattering of ourselves by saying, "Christ has paid the ransom and made satisfaction for sin," and that *he died for our sins*, is a false and vain comfort if we also do not *die from sin* in him, and put on his *merit* in *new obedience*, and live within it.

37. He who is a bitter *enemy* and *hater* of *sin* can and may comfort himself with the *sufferings of Christ*. He who does not willingly *see*, *hear*, or *taste sin*, but is at *enmity* with it, and would willingly *always* do that which is well and right, if he knew

38. But the outward flattery of being considered a child of GOD by imputation or external application is false and vain. The *work* done in, or by, the *outward flesh* alone does not make the *child of God*; rather, the *working* of Christ in the *spirit* makes, and indeed *is*, the *child of God*. This inward *working* is so powerful that it shines forth as a *new light* in the outward *life*, and proves itself to be the *child of God* by its external conduct and actions.

39. For if the *eye* of the soul is *light*, then the whole body is *light* in all its members. Now if anyone boasts that he is the *child of God*, and yet allows the *body* to burn in *sins*, he is no *true child*, nor is he capable of the *inheritance*; instead, he lies bound by the *chains* of the *devil* in gross *darkness*. And if he does not find in himself an *earnest* and sincere *desire* for *well-doing* in *love*, then his pretense to the status of a child is but an invention of *reason* proceeding from *self*. He cannot see GOD unless he *is born anew* and shows by his *power* and *life* that he is *His true child*. For there is no *fire* that does not have *light* in it; and if the *divine fire* be in the mind, it will shine forth, and the mind will do that which GOD wants to be done.

40. But perhaps you will say, "I have a will indeed to do so; I would willingly do it, but I am so hindered that I cannot."

41. No, you vile man, GOD draws you to be *His child*, but you *will not*; the *soft cushion* in evil is dearer to you than to be so readily parted with. You prefer the *joy* of wickedness to the *joy of GOD*. You are still wholly swallowed up in *self* and live according to the *law of sin*, and *that* hinders you. You are unwilling to *die* to the pleasures of the *flesh*, and therefore you are not in the <term>filiation</term>. GOD draws you to it, but you yourself *will not*.

42. O, how fine a thing *Adam* would think it if he might be taken into *heaven* with this *will* of the voluptuous *flesh* about him, and have the *child of wickedness*, which is full of deceit, set upon the throne of GOD. *Lucifer* also would gladly have had it so, but he *was cast out*.

43. It is a troublesome thing to *mortify* the *evil will*; no one is willing to do it. We would all gladly be the *children of God* if we might be so with this rough garment of *fallen nature* about us. But that cannot be. *This world passes away*, and the *outward life* must die; what good can the *adoption* in the *mortal body of flesh and blood only* do me?

44. If we would inherit the <term>filiation</term>, we must also put on the *New Man* which can inherit it, as being *like the Deity*. GOD

<meta>catchword: GOD</meta>

He rebukes those who cling to sinful pleasures while pretending to be spiritual, stating that the evil will must be mortified to achieve true sonship.</summary>

<header>The Way to Christ.</header>

will have no *sinners* in *heaven*, but only those who are *born anew* and become *children*, and thus have put on *heaven*.

45. Therefore, it is not as easy a matter to become a *child of God* as people imagine. Indeed, it is not a troublesome thing to one who has put on <term>filiation</term> <note>the state of being a son or child of God</note>, whose *light shines*; for it is *joy* to such a person. But to turn the mind and destroy *self*, a *strong* and *continued earnestness* is required, along with such a *sturdy* and *steady purpose* that even if the body and soul should be torn apart by it, the *will* would persevere constantly and not return to *self*.

46. A person must wrestle until the *dark center*, which is shut tight, breaks open and the *spark* lying within *kindles*; and from there, the *noble lily-branch* immediately sprouts, as from the *divine grain of mustard seed*, as *Christ* says. A person must pray earnestly, with great *humility*, and for a while become a *fool* in his own *reason*, seeing himself as void of *understanding* until *Christ* is formed in this *new incarnation*.

47. And then when *Christ* is born, *Herod* is ready to kill the *child*, which he seeks to do *outwardly* through persecutions and *inwardly* through temptations, to test whether this *lily-branch* will be strong enough to destroy the *kingdom* of the *devil*, which is manifested in the *flesh*.

48. Then this *Destroyer of the Serpent* is brought into the *wilderness* after being *baptized with the Holy Spirit*, and is tempted and tried to see whether or not he will *continue* in *resignation* to the *will* of GOD. In this temptation, he must stand so firm that, if necessary, he would leave *all earthly things*, and even his *outward life*, to be a *child of God*.

49. No *temporal honor* must be preferred before <term>filiation</term>. Rather, he must with his *will* leave and forsake it all, not accounting it *his own*, but esteeming himself only as a *servant* in it, who is to obey his *master*. *He must leave all worldly property*. We do not mean that he may not *have* or *possess* anything; but his *heart* must forsake it, not bringing *his will* into it, nor counting it *his own*. For if he sets his *heart* upon it, he has no *power* to serve those who are in need with it.

50. *Self* is but a *slave* to its temporal possessions, but *resignation* has rule over all that is under it. *Self* must do what the *devil* wants it to do in *fleshly voluptuousness* and *pride of life*; but *resignation* treads it all under with the *feet of the mind*. *Self* despises that which is *lowly* and *simple*; but *resignation* sits down with the *lowly* in the *dust*. It says,

> ‘I will be simple in myself and understand nothing, lest my
> ‘understanding should exalt itself and sin. I will lie down in

I will sleep in myself until the Lord awakens me with his Spirit; and if he will not, then I will look up to him in silence and await his commands.'

51. Beloved brethren, men at this time boast much of *faith*; but where is it to be found? The *modern faith* is nothing but the *history*. Where is that *child* who *believes* that *Jesus* is born? If that *child* were in existence, and did *believe* that *Jesus* is born, it would also draw near to the *sweet child Jesus*, and *receive* him and *nurse* him.

52. Alas! The *faith* of *this day* is but *historical*, a mere assent to the matter of fact that *Jesus Christ* lived and died, that the *Jews* killed him, that he left *this world*, and is not *king on earth* in the outward *man*; but that men may do what they want, and need not *die* from *sin* and their evil *lusts*. All this the wicked child *self* rejoices in, so that it may fatten the *devil* by *living self-indulgently*.

53. This shows plainly that *true faith* was never weaker since *Christ's* time than it is *now*. When nevertheless the *world* cries aloud and says, 'We have the true faith;' and contend about a *child* with a *contention* worse than any since men were on earth.

54. If you are truly *Zion* and have that *newborn child* which was lost and is found again, then let it be seen in *power* and *virtue*. Let us all openly see the *sweet child Jesus* brought forth by you, and that you are his *nurse*. If not, then the *children in Christ* will say you have found nothing but the *cradle of the child*—that is, the *history*.

55. Where do you have the *sweet child Jesus*, you who are so exalted with the *history* and with your *false* and *seeming faith*? O how the *child Jesus* will visit you one day in the *Father's property*, the property of *anger*, in your own <term>Turba</term> which you have fattened! He calls you now in *love*, but you will not hear, for your ears are stopped with *covetousness* and *voluptuousness*. Therefore, the *sound* of the *trumpet* shall one day alarm you with the *hard thunderclap* of your *Turba* and rouse you up, if perhaps you will then seek and find the *sweet child Jesus*.

56. Beloved brethren, this is a *time* of seeking, of seeking and of *finding*. It is a *time of earnestness*; whom it touches, it touches *home*. He who *watches* shall hear and see it; but he who *sleeps* in sin and says in the fat days of his belly, 'All is peace and quiet; we hear no sound from the Lord,' shall be

### OF
# REGENERATION,
### OR THE
# NEW BIRTH,
### SHOWING

How he that *earnestly* seeks *Salvation* must allow himself to be brought out of the *confused* and *contentious Babel* by the Spirit of Christ, so that he may be born anew in the Spirit of Christ and live to *Him* alone.

->(In the Year 1622.)<-

By *JACOB BOEHME*

<meta>This preface follows the scriptural call to leave "Babylon" by providing a simplified summary of regeneration for the "simple Children of Christ," while directing more advanced readers to the author's deeper theological works for a more exhaustive treatment of his philosophy.</meta>

# The Author’s Preface
## to the Reader.

ALTHOUGH I have in my other *Writings* set down a clear description of *Regeneration*, or the *New Birth*, from its *Foundation*; yet because not everyone has them, nor does everyone have the *Capacity* to understand them; I have therefore, as a service to the simple *Children of Christ*, set down here a short *Summary* concerning the *New Birth*.

But if anyone desires to search the *deep Foundation* from which everything flows, and has the *Gift* to understand it, let them read these following *Books*.

1. *The Three Principles of the Divine Essence.*
2. *The Threefold Life of Man.*
3. *The Forty Questions of the Original Essence, Substance, Nature, and Property of the Soul.*
4. *The Incarnation and Birth of Jesus Christ the Son of God; also of his Suffering, Death, and Resurrection.*
5. The *six Points* treating of the *Three Worlds*, how they are in one another as *one*; and yet make *Three Principles, namely, Three Births or Centers.*
6. *The Mysterium Magnum*, which is an *Interpretation* of *Genesis*.

And in them he shall find *everything* he can ask, and that as *deeply* as the Mind of Man is able to reach. I have written this for the *true Israelites*, that is, for the *Hungry and Thirsty Hearts* that long for the *Fountain of Christ*, who are my *Fellow Members* in the *Spirit of Christ*: But not for the *Ishmaelites* and *Scorners*, for they have a *Book* within them, with which they vex, persecute, and suppress the *Children of Christ* who are under the *Cross*; and yet, though it is unwillingly and unknowingly to themselves, they must be *Servants* to such *Children of Christ*.

<summary>Jacob Boehme introduces this work as a simplified guide to spiritual regeneration for ordinary believers while listing his more complex volumes for those seeking deeper insight. He distinguishes between sincere seekers, whom he calls "true Israelites," and those who persecute the faithful.</summary>
<keywords>Jacob Boehme, Regeneration, New Birth, Divine Essence, spiritual foundation, Israelites, Ishmaelites, Mysterium Magnum, theology</keywords>

<meta>This begins Chapter I of the treatise, moving from the author's introductory list of deeper works to the fundamental spiritual requirements of the New Birth, as taught in the Gospels.</meta>

# OF REGENERATION
---
## Chapter I.
->*Showing how Man should consider himself.*<-

CHRIST said, “Unless you turn and become as children, you shall not see the Kingdom of God.” Again, he said to *Nicodemus*; “Unless a man be born again, of *water* and of the *Spirit*, he cannot enter into the Kingdom of God; for that which is born of the flesh is flesh, and that which is born of the Spirit is spirit.”

2. Also the *Scripture* positively declares, that “the *fleshly natural man* does not receive the things of the Spirit of God, for they are foolishness to him, nor can he know or conceive them.”

3. Now seeing that all of us have *flesh* and *blood* and are *mortal*, as we find by *experience*, and yet the *Scripture* says, that “we are the *temples of the Holy Ghost*, who dwells in us,” and that “the *Kingdom of God* is within us,” and that “*Christ* must be formed in us;” also that “He will give us his flesh for food, and his blood for drink:” and that, “whoever shall not eat of the flesh of the Son of Man, and drink his blood has no life in him.” Therefore we should seriously consider, what kind of *Man* it is in us, that is capable of being thus like the *Deity*.

4. For it cannot be said of the *mortal flesh* that turns to *earth* again, and lives in the *vanity of this world*, and continually lusts against GOD, that it is the *temple of the Holy Ghost*; much less can it be said that the *New Birth* comes to pass in this *earthly flesh*, which *dies* and *putrefies*, and is a continual *House of Sin*.

5. Yet seeing that it remains certain, that a *true Christian is born of Christ*, and that the *New Birth* is the *temple of the Holy Ghost* which dwells in us, and that the *New Man* only, who is born of Christ, partakes of the *flesh* and *blood of Christ*; it appears that it is not such an easy *matter to be a Christian*.

6. And that *Christianity* does not consist in the mere *knowing of the history*, and applying the *knowledge* of it to ourselves, saying that “Christ died for us, and has destroyed death and turned it into life in us, and that He has paid the ransom for us, so that we need do nothing but comfort ourselves with it, and steadfastly believe that it is so.”

7. For we find in ourselves that *sin is living, lusting, and strong*,

<summary>This chapter establishes the necessity of a spiritual "New Birth," arguing that the mortal, sinful flesh cannot serve as the temple of the Holy Spirit. It emphasizes that true Christianity requires a literal transformation rather than a mere historical belief in Christ's sacrifice.</summary>
<keywords>regeneration, New Birth, Nicodemus, flesh vs spirit, temple of the Holy Ghost, inner kingdom, Jacob Boehme</keywords>

--- Page 87 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the nature of the New Birth, clarifying the distinction between the mortal, sinful flesh and the spiritual New Man born of Christ.</meta>

and powerfully *working* in the *flesh*; therefore it must be something else, which does not cooperate with *sin* in the *flesh*, nor *wills* it, and that is the *New Birth* in *Christ*.

8. For *St. Paul* says, “There is no condemnation to those who are in *Christ Jesus*.” And further, “Should we who are *Christians* still be *sinners*? *God* forbid, seeing we are dead to *sin in Christ*.”

9. Besides, the *Man of Sin* cannot be the “*temple* of the *Holy Spirit*”; and yet “there is no *man* who does not sin, for *God* has shut up everyone under *sin*.” As the *Scripture* says, “No one living is righteous in Your sight, if You impute his *sins* to him.” The righteous *man* falls seven *times a day*; and yet it cannot mean that the righteous part falls and sins, but rather his mortal and sinful *man*. For the righteousness of a *Christian* in *Christ* cannot *sin*.

10. Moreover, *St. Paul* says, “Our *citizenship* <note>The original text uses "conversation," which in the 17th century referred to one's conduct, way of life, or citizenship.</note> is in *heaven*, from where we expect our *Savior Jesus Christ*.” Now, if our *citizenship* is in *heaven*, then *heaven* must be in us; *Christ* dwells in *heaven*, and if we are His *temple*, then that *temple* of *heaven* must be in us.

11. But despite all this, seeing as *sin* tempts us within ourselves, whereby the *devil* has access to us within, therefore *hell* must also be in us too, for the *devil* dwells in *hell*; wherever he is, he is in *hell* and cannot come out of it. Yes, when he possesses a *man*, he dwells in *hell*, namely, in the *anger of God* in that *man*.

12. Therefore we ought to consider well what *man* is, and how he is a *man*; and then we shall find that a true *Christian* is not a mere *historical new man*—as if it were enough for us to outwardly confess *Christ* and believe that He is the *Son of God* and has paid the *ransom* for us. For *righteousness* is of no use if it is only imputed from without—that is, by believing only that it is so *imputed*. But it is an *inherent righteousness* born in us, by which we become the *children of God*, that is of value.

13. And as the *earthly flesh* must die, so also the *life* and *will* must die to *sin* and be as a *child* that knows nothing, but longs only for the *mother* who brought it forth. So likewise must the *will* of a *Christian* enter again into its *mother*, namely, into the *Spirit of Christ*, and become a *child* in itself—in its *own will* and *power*—having its *will* and *desire* inclined and directed only toward its *mother*. And a *new will* and *obedience in righteousness*, which *wills sin* no more, must rise from *death* out of the *Spirit of Christ* in him.

14. For that *will* is not born anew which desires and admits *vanity* into itself; and yet there remains a *will*

<summary>The author explains that a true Christian possesses an inherent righteousness born of the Spirit, distinguishing it from the sinful mortal flesh. He emphasizes that heaven and hell both exist within the human experience, and regeneration requires the human will to die to sin and be reborn into the Spirit of Christ.</summary>

<keywords>New Birth, Regeneration, Inherent Righteousness, St. Paul, Temple of the Holy Spirit, Man of Sin, Heaven within, Hell within, Will of Christ, Imputed Righteousness</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the nature of the New Birth, explaining how the spiritual man exists within the physical body without being part of its sinful nature.</meta>

# Of Regeneration.

which longs after *vanity*, and sins, even in the *new-born* or *regenerate man*. Therefore the *image* or *nature* of *man* should be well understood, and how the *new-birth* comes to pass; seeing it is not wrought in the *mortal flesh*, and yet is wrought truly and really *in* us, in *flesh* and *blood*, in *water* and *spirit*, as the *Scripture* says.

15. We should therefore rightly understand what kind of *man* it is in us who is the *member of Christ* and *temple of God* who dwells in *heaven*. And then also what kind of *man* it is that the *devil* rules and drives; for he cannot meddle with the *temple of Christ*, nor does he care much for the *mortal flesh*; and yet there are not *three* men in one another, for all make but *one man*.

16. Now if we want to understand this rightly, we must consider <unclear>Time</unclear> and *eternity*, and how they are in one another; also *light* and *darkness*, *good* and *evil*; but especially the *original* of *man*, which may be understood in this way.

17. The *outward world* with the stars and four elements, in which *man* and all *creatures* live, is neither *God*, nor is it called *God*. Indeed *God* dwells *in* it, but the *substance* of the *outward world* does not comprehend him.

18. We also see that the *light* shines in *darkness*, and the *darkness* does not comprehend the *light*, and yet they both dwell in one another. The four elements are also an example of this; which in their original are but *one* element, which is neither hot nor cold, nor dry nor moist, and yet by its stirring separates itself into four *properties*, *namely*, into *fire*, *air*, *water*, and *earth*.

19. Who would believe that *fire* produces or generates *water*? And that the original of *fire* could be in *water*, if we did not see it with our eyes in tempests of thunder, lightning, and *rain*; and did not find also that in living creatures, the essential *fire* of the body dwells in the *blood*, and that the *blood* is the *mother* of the *fire*, and the *fire* is the *father* of the *blood*.

<warning>Smudge on paragraph number</warning>

20. And as God dwells in the *world* and fills all things, and yet possesses nothing; and as the *fire* dwells *in water*, and yet does not possess it; also, as the *light* dwells in *darkness*, and yet does not possess the *darkness*; as the *day* is in the *night*, and the *night* in the *day*, *time* in *eternity*, and *eternity* in *time*; so is *man* created according to the *outward humanity*; he is the *time*, and *in* the time, and the time is the *outward world*, and it is also the *outward man*.

21. The *inward man* is *eternity* and the *spiritual time* and *world*, which also consists of *light* and *darkness*, *namely*, of the *love* of God, as to the *eternal light*, and of the *anger* of God
