<summary>This page explains the dual nature of humanity, distinguishing between the outward man governed by the elements of time and the inward man belonging to eternity. It uses metaphors of light in darkness and fire in water to illustrate how God's spiritual presence inhabits the physical world without being consumed by it.</summary>

<keywords>regeneration, new birth, outward man, inward man, elements, eternity, time, fire and water, light and darkness, temple of God</keywords>

--- Page 89 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on how the spiritual man relates to the forces of light and darkness within the soul, explaining the coexistence of these opposing states.</meta>

<page-num>66</page-num>
<header>The Way to Christ.</header>

---

as to the eternal *Darkness*; whichever of these is manifest in him, his Spirit dwells in that, whether it be *Darkness* or *Light*.

22. For *Light* and *Darkness* are both *in* him, but each of them dwells in itself, and neither of them possesses the other; but if one of them enters into the other, and wants to possess it, then that other loses its Right and Power.

23. The *passive* loses its Power; for if the *Light* is made manifest in the *Darkness*, then the *Darkness* loses its *Darkness*, and is not known or discerned. Also on the contrary, if the *Darkness* arises in the *Light* and gets the Upper-hand, then the *Light* and its power are extinguished. This is to be observed also in *Man*.

24. The *eternal Darkness* of the Soul is Hell, *namely*, an aching *Source* of *Anguish*, which is called the *Anger of God*; but the *eternal Light* in the Soul is the Kingdom of Heaven, where <unclear>the</unclear> fiery *Anguish* of *Darkness* is changed into *Joy*.

25. For the same *Nature* of *Anguish*, which in the *Darkness* is a *Cause* of *Sadness*, is in the *Light* a *Cause* of the outward and stirring *Joy*. For the *Source* or Original in *Light*, and the *Source* in *Darkness* are but *one eternal Source*, and *one Nature*, and yet they, *namely*, the *Light* and *Darkness*, have a mighty Difference in the *Source*; the one dwells *in* the other and begets the other, and yet is not the other. The *Fire* is *painful* and *consuming*, but the *Light* is *yielding, friendly, powerful*, and *delightful*, a sweet and amiable *Joy*.

26. This may be found also in *Man*; he is and lives in *three Worlds*; the *First* is the *eternal dark World, namely*, the *Center* of the *eternal Nature* which produces or generates the *Fire, namely*, the *Source* or *Property* of *Anguish*.

27. The *second* is the *eternal light World*, which begets the *eternal Joy*, which is the *Divine Habitation* wherein the *Spirit* of God dwells, and wherein the *Spirit of Christ* receives the *human Substance*, and subdues the *Darkness*, so that it must be a *Cause* of *Joy* in the *Spirit of Christ* in the *Light*.

28. The *Third* is the outward visible *World* in the four <unclear>Elements</unclear> and the visible *Stars*; though indeed every Element has its peculiar Constellation in itself, from which the *Desire* and *Property* arise, and is like a *Mind*.

29. Thus you may understand, that the *Fire* in the *Light* is a *Fire of Love*, a *Desire* for *Meekness* and *Delightfulness*; but the *Fire* in the *Darkness* is a *Fire of Anguish*, and is painful, irksome, hostile, and full of *Contrariety* in its *Essence*. The *Fire* of the *Light* has a good Relish or Taste, but the Taste in the *Essence* of *Darkness* is unpleasant, loathsome, and irksome. For all the *Forms* or *Properties* in the eternal *Nature*, until they reach to *Fire*, are in great *Anguish*.

<summary>This page describes the dual nature of the soul as containing both light (heavenly joy) and darkness (hellish anguish), explaining how these forces coexist and interact within man. It further identifies three "worlds" that man inhabits: the eternal dark world, the eternal light world of God, and the visible physical world.</summary>
<keywords>Light and Darkness, eternal Nature, three Worlds, Spirit of Christ, Fire of Love, Fire of Anguish, Anger of God, human Substance, Elements</keywords>

## Chapter II.
### How Man is created.

HERE we are to consider the *Creation of Man*. *Moses* says, "God created Man in His Image, in the Image of God created He him." This we understand to be both out of the *eternal* and *temporal Birth*; out of the *inward* and *spiritual World* which he breathed into him, into the *created Image*; and then out of the *Substance* of the *inward spiritual World*, which is *Holy*.

31. For as there is a *Nature* and *Substance* in the *outward* <unclear>World</unclear>; so also in the *inward spiritual World* there is a *Nature* and *Substance* which is *Spiritual*; from which the *outward World* is breathed forth and produced out of *Light* and *Darkness*, and created to have a *Beginning* and *Time*.

32. And out of the *Substance* of the *inward and outward World* *Man was created*; out of and in the *Likeness* of the *Birth* of all *Substances*. The *Body* is a <term>Limbus</term> (an *Extract* or a kind of *Seed*, which contains all that which the *Thing* from which it is taken has) of the *Earth*, and also a <term>Limbus</term> of the *Heavenly Substance*; for the *Earth* is breathed forth, spoken out, or created out of the *dark and light World*. In the *Word Fiat* (or *creating Word*), namely, in the *eternal Desire, Man* was taken out of the *Earth*, and so *created an Image out of Time and Eternity*.

33. This *Image* was in the *inward* and *spiritual Element*, from which the *four Elements* proceed and are produced. In that *one Element* was *Paradise*; for the *Properties* of *Nature* from the *Fire-dark-and-light-World* were all in *Harmony* and *Agreement* in *Number*, *Weight*, and *Measure*. One of them was not *manifested* more prominently than another; therefore, there was no *Frailty* therein. For no one *Property* was predominant over <unclear>another</unclear>, nor was there any *Strife* or *Contrariety* among the *Powers* and *Properties*.

34. Into this *created Image* GOD breathed the *Spirit* and *Breath of Understanding* out of the *three Worlds*, as one only *Soul*, which, as to its *Original Principle* or *Essence*, is, or consists in, the *inward dark Fire-World* of the *eternal spiritual Nature*; according to which GOD calls himself a *strong jealous God*, and a *consuming Fire*.

35. *And this now is the eternal creaturely great Soul, a Magical Breath of Fire, in which Fire consists the Original of Life, from the great Power of variation.* GOD'S *Anger*, or the

<summary>This page explains that Man was created as an image of both the eternal spiritual world and the temporal world, containing the "Limbus" or essence of both heaven and earth. It describes the original human soul as a harmonious balance of natural properties breathed into existence by God.</summary>

36. The *second Property* of the *Breath* of GOD is the *Spirit* of the *Source* of *Light*, proceeding from the great *fiery Desire* of *Love*, from great *Meekness*; according to which GOD calls himself a *loving, merciful* GOD; in which consists the true *Spirit* of *Understanding*, and of *Life* in *Power*.

37. For as *Light* shines from *Power*, and as the *Power* of *Understanding* is discerned in the *Light*, so the *Breath* of the *Light* was joined to the *Breath* of the *Fire* of GOD, and breathed into the *Image* of *Man*.

38. The *third Property* of the *Breath* of GOD was the *outward Air* with its *Constellation* or *Astrum*, in which the *Life* and *Constellation* of the *outward Substance* and *Body* consisted. This He breathed into his Nostrils; and as *Time* and *Eternity* are linked together; and as *Time* is produced out of *Eternity*, so the *inward Breath* of GOD was joined to the *outward*.

39. This *three-fold Soul* was at once breathed into *Man*; and each *Substance* of the *Body* received the *Spirit* according to its *Property*. The *outward Flesh* received the *outward Air* and its *Constellations*, for a *rational* and *vegetative Life*, for the *manifestation* of the *Wonders* of GOD; and the *Light Body* or *Heavenly Substance* received the *Breath* of the *Light* of the great *Divine Powers* and *Virtues*; which *Breath* is called the *Holy Ghost*.

40. Thus the *Light* pierced through the *Darkness, namely*, through the *dark Breath* of *Fire*, and also through the *Breath* of the *outward Air* and its *Constellation* or *Astrum*, and so deprived all the *Properties* of their *Power*, so that neither the *Anguish* of the *Breath* of *Fire* in the *inward Property* of the *Soul*, nor *Heat* nor *Cold*, nor any of all the *Properties* of the *outward Constellation*, might or could be manifested.

41. The *Properties* of all the *three Worlds* in *Soul* and *Body* were in equal *Agreement, Balance*, and *Weight*. That which was *inward* and *Holy* ruled through and over the *outward*, that is, the *outward Parts* of the *outward Life*, of the *outward Stars* or *Constellations* and the *four Elements*; and that *original* and *universal Power* of the *inward* over the *outward* constituted the *Holy Paradise*.

42. And thus *Man* was both in *Heaven* and also in the *outward World*, and was *Lord* over all the *Creatures* of this *World*. Nothing could destroy him.

43. For such was the *Earth* also, until the *Curse* of GOD broke forth. The *Holy Property* of the *Spiritual World* sprang up through the *Earth*, and brought forth *Holy Paradisaical*

44. And he had no need for *teeth* or *entrails* in his *body*. For just as the *light* swallows up *darkness*, and as the *fire* devours *water* and yet is not *filled* by it, man also had such a *center* for his *mouth* to eat with, according to the *manner of eternity*.

45. And he could also *generate* his *kind* out of *himself*, without any *dividing* or *opening* of his *body* and *spirit*, in the same way that GOD *generated* the outward *world*. God did not *divide* himself; but in his *desire*—that is, in the Word *Fiat*—he manifested himself and brought that same *desire* into a *form* according to the eternal *spiritual birth*. So also *man* was created as an *image* and *likeness* of GOD in that respect, according to *time* and *eternity*, out of both *time* and *eternity*, yet for an *immortal life* which was without enmity or conflict.

46. But the *Devil*, having been a *prince* and *hierarch* in the place of *this world* and cast out for his *pride* into the *dark*, *anguished*, *painful*, and *hostile property* and *source*—into the *wrath of God*—envied *man* the *glory* of being created in and for the *spiritual world*, the place which he himself once possessed. Therefore, he brought his *imagination* or *desire* into the *image of man* and made it so *lustful* that the *dark world*, and also the *outward world*, arose in *man*. They departed from the equal *agreement* and *balance* in which they stood, and so one predominated over the other.

47. And then the *properties* were each made *separately* manifest in themselves, and each of them *lusted* after what was *like* itself. That which came from the birth of the *dark world*, and also that which came from the birth of the *light world*, each wanted to *eat* of the *limbus* <note>The borders or outer regions, here referring to the essence or substance of the earth.</note> of the *earth* according to its *hunger*; and so *evil* and *good* became manifest in *Adam*.

48. And when the *hunger* of the *properties* went into the *earth*, from which the properties of the body were extracted, then the *Fiat* drew such a *branch* out of the *earth* as the *properties* could eat of in their *awakened vanity*; for this was possible.

49. For the *spirit* of the strong and great *magical power* of *time* and *eternity* was in *Adam*, from which the *earth* with its *properties* was breathed forth; and so the *Fiat*—that is, the strong *desire* of the eternal *nature*—attracted the *essence of the earth*. And thus GOD let the *Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil* grow for *Adam*, according to his *awakened properties*; for the great *power* of the *soul* and of the *body* caused it.

50. Then *man* had to be tested to see whether he would stand and persist in *his own* powers before the tempter, the *devil*, and before the *wrath* of *eternal nature*. It was to be seen if the soul would continue in the balanced agreement of its *properties* in true resignation under God's Spirit—acting as an *instrument* of God's harmony, a tuned instrument of divine *joy* for the *Spirit* of God to play upon. This was tested by *that tree*, and this severe commandment was added: "You shall not eat of it, for on the day that you eat of it, you shall surely die."

51. But since God *knew* that *man* would not stand, and that he had already imagined and lusted after *good* and *evil*, God said, "It is not good for man to be alone; we will make a helper suitable for him."

52. For God saw that *Adam* could no longer *generate magically*, having entered into *vanity* through his lust. Therefore, *Moses* says, "God caused a deep sleep to fall upon him, and he slept." This means that because *man* would not remain in the obedience of divine harmony within his *properties*—submitting himself to stand still as an *instrument* of the *Spirit* of God—God allowed him to fall from the *divine harmony* into a harmony of *his own*, namely, into the awakened properties of *evil* and *good*. The *spirit* of his soul entered into these.

53. In *this sleep*, he died to the *angelic world* and fell under the power of the *outward Fiat*. Thus, he said farewell to the *eternal image* that was begotten of God. Here, his *angelic form* and *power* fell into a *faint* and lay upon the ground.

54. Then, by the *Fiat*, God made *woman* out of him—out of the *Matrix* of *Venus*, which is the *property* in which *Adam* had the generative power within himself. Thus, out of *one* body He made *two*, and divided the *properties* of the *tinctures*—specifically, the watery and fiery constellations in the element. This division was not entirely in *substance*, but in the *spirit*—that is, the properties of the *watery* and *fiery* soul.

55. And yet it is still only *one* thing; only the property of the *tincture* was divided. The *desire* of *self-love* was taken out of *Adam* and formed into a *woman* according to his likeness. This is why *man* now so eagerly desires the *matrix* of the *woman*, and the *woman* desires the *limbus* of the *man*—that is, the *fire-element*, the *origin* of the true soul, meaning the tincture of *fire*. For these two were one in *Adam*, and in that unity consisted the *magical begetting*.

56. As soon as *Eve* was made out of *Adam* during his *sleep*, both *Adam* and *Eve* were at that instant placed and established in

57. Of this, the poor soul that is captivated in *vanity* is *ashamed* today, and sorry that its body has taken such a *bestial*, monstrous shape. Nothing can be clearer than this. For it is because mankind are *ashamed* of their members and nakedness that they *borrow* their clothing from the *earthly creatures*. They would not have done this had they not lost the *angelic form* and assumed that of a *beast*.

58. This *borrowed* clothing, together with the awakened *earthliness* and *subjection* to the powers of heat and cold, is a plain and full proof to man that he is not truly *at home in this world*. For all *earthly appetites*, *cares*, and *fears*, together with this *false clothing*, must *perish* and be severed from the soul again.

59. Now when *Adam* awoke from *sleep*, he beheld his *wife* and *knew* that she came out of him; for he had not yet eaten of *vanity* with his *outward mouth*, but only with the *imagination*, *desire*, and *lust*.

60. And it was the *first desire of Eve* that she might eat of the Tree of Vanity, of *evil* and *good*, to which the *devil* in the form of a *serpent* persuaded her, saying "that her eyes should be opened, and she should be as God himself"; which was both a *lie* and a *truth*.

61. But he did not tell her that she would lose the *divine light* and *power* by doing so; he only said "her eyes should be opened, that she might taste, prove, and know evil and good, as he had done." Neither did he tell her that *heat* and *cold* would awaken in her, and that the *property of the outward constellations* would have great power over the *flesh* and over the *mind*.

62. His only aim was that the *angelic image*—namely, the *substance* which came from the *inward spiritual world*—might *disappear* in them. For then they would be constrained to live in *subjection* to the *gross earthliness* and the *constellations* or stars; and then he knew well enough that when the *outward world* perished, the soul would be with him in *darkness*. For he saw that the body must die, as he perceived from what God had indicated; and so he still expected to be *lord* to all eternity in the *place of this world*, in his false shape which he had acquired; and therefore he seduced *man*.

63. For when *Adam* and *Eve* were eating the *fruit*, *evil* and *good*, into the body, then the *imagination* of the *body* received *vanity* in the *fruit*, and then *vanity* awakened in the *flesh*, and the *dark world* got the upper hand and dominion in the *vanity*

## Chapter III.

->Of the lamentable *fall of man*, and of the *means of his deliverance*.<-

NOW when *Adam* and *Eve* fell into this *vanity*, then the *wrath of nature* awoke in every *property*, and through the *desire* it impressed the *vanity* of the *earthliness* and the *wrath of God* into itself.

68. And then the *flesh* became *gross* and *rough*, like the *flesh* of a *beast*. The *soul* was captivated in that *essence* and saw that its *body* had become a *beast* and had acquired *bestial members* for reproduction, and a filthy *carcass* into which *desire* would stuff the *loathsomeness* of which it was ashamed in the presence of God <unclear>—</unclear> and therefore *Adam* and *Eve* hid themselves under the trees of the *garden of Eden*. *Heat* and *cold* also seized them.

69. And here the *heaven* in man trembled with horror, just as the *earth* quaked in wrath when this *anger* was destroyed on the *cross* by the sweet *love of God*; there, the *anger trembled* before the sweet *love of God*.

70. And for the sake of this *vanity* which was thus awakened in man, God cursed the *earth*, lest the *holy element* should spring or shine forth any more through the outward *fruit* and bring forth *paradisaical fruit*. For there was then no *creature* that

72. Now when God saw that his *fair image* was spoiled, he manifested himself to fallen *Adam* and *Eve* and had *pity* on them, and promised himself to them as an everlasting possession; and that with his great *love* in the *received humanity*, he would destroy the *power of the serpentine property—namely*, the *vanity* in the *wrath of God* awakened in them. And this was the *breaking of the head of the serpent* which he would perform—*namely*, he would destroy the *dark death* and subdue the *anger* with his great *love*.

73. 74. And the *offering* was a *figure* of the *restitution* of that which *Adam* had lost, and so God *expiated* his *anger* in the *human property* through the *offering* within the *limit* of the *covenant*.

75. In this *covenant*, the most holy, sweet name *Jesus*, proceeding out of the holy name and great *power Jehovah*, had incorporated itself, so that he would again move and manifest himself in the *substance* of the *heavenly world* which disappeared in *Adam*, and kindle the holy *divine life* within it once more.

76. This *mark* or *limit* of the *covenant* was propagated from *Adam* and his children, from man to man, and passed through from one to all; just as *sin* and the awakened *vanity* also passed through from one to all.

77. And it stood in the *promise* of the *covenant* at the *end*, in the *root of David* in the *Virgin Mary*, who was—in the *inward kingdom* of the *hidden humanity* (*namely*, of the *essentiality* that disappeared regarding the *kingdom of God*)—the daughter of God’s *covenant*. But in the *outward* sense, according to her natural *humanity*, she was begotten by her true bodily father *Joachim* and her true mother *Anna*, out of the *essences* and *substance* of their souls and bodies, like all other children of *Adam*; a true daughter of *Eve*.

78. In this *Mary*, from the *Virgin* (*namely*, the *Wisdom of God*) in the promised *limit* of the *covenant*, of which all the prophets

80. And so now God's *substance*, in which he dwells and works, and the *disappeared substance* of man, have become *one person*; for the *holy divine substance* *anointed* the *disappeared substance*. Therefore, that *person* is called *Christus*, the *Anointed of God*.

81. And this is the *dry rod of Aaron* which *blossomed* and *bore almonds*, and the true *High Priest*. It is *that humanity* of which *Christ* spoke when he said that "he had come from heaven and was in heaven," and that "no man could ascend into heaven except the Son of Man who has come from heaven and is in heaven."

82. Now when he says, "He has come from heaven," it refers to the *heavenly substance*, the *heavenly corporeality*. For the *power* and *virtue* of God needs no *coming* anywhere, since it is everywhere altogether immeasurable and undivided. But *substance* needs *coming*; the *power* or *virtue* needs to *move* itself and *manifest* itself in *substance*.

83. And *that substance* entered into the *human substance* and received it; not only *that part* of *heavenly substance* which disappeared in *Adam*, but the *whole human essence* in *soul* and *flesh*, according to all *three worlds*.

84. But he has not received or taken upon himself the awakened or impressed *vanity* which the *devil*, through his *imagination*, brought into the *flesh* (by which the *flesh* committed *sin*). Though he has indeed taken upon himself the awakened *forms of life* as they had strayed from their equal agreement, each of them into its own *desire*.

85. For therein lay our *infirmity* and the *death* which he was to drown with his *heavenly holy blood*. In this way, *he* took upon himself all our *sins* and *infirmities*, as well as *death* and *hell* in

# Chapter IV

## *How we are born anew, and how we may fall into God’s anger again.*

Now here we may rightly understand what our *new birth*, or *regeneration*, is; and how we may become, and continue to be, the *temple of God*; even though in this lifetime, according to the *outward humanity*, we are *sinful mortal men*.

88. *Christ in the human essence* has broken open the *gates* of our *inward heavenly humanity*, which was shut up in *Adam*. So nothing is now lacking, except that the *soul* draw its *will* out from the *vanity* of the corrupted *flesh*, and bring it into this *open gate* in the *spirit of Christ*.

89. *Great* and *strong earnestness* is required here; and not only a *learning* and *knowing*, but a real *hunger* and *thirst* after the *spirit of Christ*. For to know only is not *faith*; rather, it is a *hunger* and *thirst* after that which I lack, so that I draw it into myself and take hold of it with *desire* and *imagination*, and make it my own; this is the truth and essence of a *Christian’s faith*.

90. The *will* must go out from the *vanity of the flesh*, and willingly yield itself up to the *suffering* and *death of Christ*, and to all the reproach of *vanity*, which mocks it because it goes out from its own house in which it was born, and regards *vanity* no more, but merely desires the *love of God* in *Christ Jesus*.

• 91. In such a *hunger* and *desire* the *will* receives and imprints into itself the *spirit of Christ* with his *heavenly corporeality*; that is, the soul in its great *hunger* and *desire* takes hold of, and draws the *body of Christ*, namely, the *heavenly substantiality*, into its *disappeared image*, within which the *word* of the *power of God* is the *working*.

<summary>This page describes how Christ transformed human nature's internal "hell" into "heavenly harmony" and defines regeneration as the soul’s active, hungry pursuit of Christ’s spirit. It emphasizes that true faith is not merely intellectual knowledge but a deep spiritual desire that draws divine substance into the soul.</summary>
<keywords>regeneration, new birth, temple of God, faith, spirit of Christ, heavenly substantiality, vanity, human properties, Jacob Böhme</keywords>

<header>The Way to Christ.</header>

92. The *hunger* of the soul brings its *desire* entirely through the *bruised property* of its humanity in the *heavenly part*, which disappeared in *Adam*. The sweet *fire of love* in the *death of Christ* bruised this humanity when the *death* of that *heavenly humanity* was destroyed.

93. And so the *hunger* of the soul receives into itself—into its disappeared *corporeality*—through the *desire*, the *holy heavenly substance*, namely, *Christ’s heavenly corporeality*. This substance fills the Father everywhere and is near to all and through all things; and through that, the *disappeared heavenly body* rises in the power of God, in the sweet name *Jesus*.

94. And this raised *heavenly spiritual body* is the *member of Christ* and the *temple of the Holy Spirit*, a true mansion of the *Holy Trinity* according to *Christ’s promise*, which says, “We will come to you and make our abode in you.”

95. The *essence* of that *life eats* the *flesh of Christ* and *drinks his blood*. For the *spirit of Christ*—namely, the *Word*, which made itself visible with the *humanity of Christ* out of and in our *disappeared humanity* through the outward man of the *substance* of this world—swallows its *holy substance* into its *fiery substance*; for every *spirit* eats of its own *body*.

96. Now if the soul eats of this sweet, holy, and *heavenly food*, then it kindles itself with the great *love* in the name and power of *Jesus*. From this, its *fire of anguish* becomes a great triumph of *joy* and *glory*, and the *true sun* arises for it, wherein it is *born* to another *will*.

97. And *here* occurs the *wedding of the Lamb*, which we heartily wish that the *nominal* and *lip-Christians* might for once find by experience in themselves, and so pass from the *history* into the *substance*.

98. But the soul does not obtain this *pearl* of the *divine wisdom* and *virtue* as *its own property* during the time of this life, because it has the outward *bestial flesh* sticking to its outward man.

99. The *power* of this *pearl* of *divine wisdom espouses* itself in this *wedding of the Lamb* and sinks down into the *heavenly image*—namely, into the *substance of the heavenly man*, who is the *temple of Christ*. It does not sink into the *fire-breath* of the soul, which is still, during this whole lifetime, fast bound to the *outward kingdom*, to the bond of vanity, with the breath of the air, and is in great danger.

100. It *darts* its beams of *love* indeed very often into the soul, by which the soul receives *light*; but the *spirit of Christ* does not yield itself up to the *fire-breath* during this life’s time, but only to the *breath of light* which was extinguished in *Adam*,

<summary>This page explains how the soul's spiritual hunger allows it to receive Christ's heavenly substance, restoring the spiritual body lost in Adam and enabling the "wedding of the Lamb." It clarifies that this divine wisdom dwells in the inner heavenly image rather than the outward, animalistic flesh during this earthly life.</summary>
<keywords>regeneration, Christ's corporeality, Holy Trinity, spiritual body, wedding of the Lamb, divine wisdom, fire-breath, Adam, nominal Christians</keywords>

--- Page 100 ---
<meta>This page continues the explanation of spiritual transformation, describing how the inward spirit seeks the "fountain of Christ" while the outward man remains tied to the world's vanity.</meta>

in which the *Temple of Christ* is, for that is the *true* and *holy Heaven*.

101. Understand correctly now what the *New Birth* or *Regeneration* is, and how it comes to pass, as follows. The *outward earthly mortal man* is not *born anew* in this *lifetime*; that is, neither the *outward flesh* nor the *outward part of the soul*. They *both* continue in the *vanity* of their *wills* which woke in *Adam*. They love their *mother*, in whose *body* they live, namely, the *dominion* of this *outward world*; and therein the *birth of sin* is manifest.

102. The *outward man* in soul and flesh (we mean the *outward part of the soul*) has no *divine will*, nor does he understand anything of GOD, as the *Scripture* says: "The natural man does not perceive the things of the Spirit of GOD," etc.

103. But the *fire-breath* of the *inward world*, if it is once enlightened, understands it; it has a great *longing*, *sighing*, *hunger*, and *thirst* after the *sweet fountain of Christ*. It refreshes itself by *hungering* and *desiring* (which is the *true faith in*) the *sweet fountain of Christ* from his *new body*, from the *heavenly substantiality*, like a hungry branch in the *vine of Christ*.

104. And the reason why the *fiery soul* cannot attain to *perfection* during this *lifetime* is because it is fast bound with the *outward bond of vanity*, through which the *Devil* continually casts his venomous rays of influence upon it, and so *sifts* it that it often bites at his *bait* and *poisons* itself. From this, *misery* and *anguish* arise, so that the *noble Sophia* hides herself in the *fountain of Christ*, in the *heavenly humanity*; for she cannot draw near to *vanity*.

105. For she knew how it went with her in *Adam* when she lost her *pearl*, which is of *grace* freely bestowed again upon the *inward humanity*; therefore she is called *Sophia*, namely, the *Bride of Christ*.

106. Here she faithfully calls to *her bridegroom*, the *fiery soul*, and exhorts him to *repentance* and to the *unburdening* of himself, or *going forth* from the *abomination of vanity*.

107. And now *war* assaults the whole man. The *outward fleshly man* fights against the *inward spiritual man*, and the *spiritual* against the *fleshly*; and so man is in continual *warfare* and *strife*, full of *trouble*, *misery*, *anguish*, and *care*.

->•<-

108. The *inward spirit* says to the *fiery soul*: *O my soul! O my love! Turn, I beseech you, and go forth from vanity, or else you will lose my love and the noble pearl.*

109. Then the *outward reason* says, namely the *bestial soul*: "You are foolish; will you be a laughingstock, and the"

<summary>This page explains that regeneration occurs in the inward spirit rather than the outward man, who remains bound to earthly vanity. It describes the spiritual conflict between the outward "bestial soul" and the inward soul's longing for Christ, guided by Sophia.</summary>

'Scorn of the world? You need the outward world to maintain this life. Beauty, power, and glory are your proper happiness; in these only can you rejoice and take delight. Why will you cast yourself into anguish, misery, and reproach? Take your pleasure, which will do both your flesh and your mind good.'

110. With such *filth* the true man is often *defiled*; that is, the outward *man* defiles himself, as a sow in the mire, and obscures his noble *pearl*. For the more vain the *outward man* grows, the darker the *inward man* becomes, until at length it disappears altogether.

111. And then the fair *paradisaical tree* is gone, and it will be very hard to recover it again. For when the outward *light*, *namely*, the outward soul, is once enlightened, so that the *outward light of reason* is kindled by the *inward light*; then the outward soul commonly tends to turn *hypocrite* and consider itself *divine*, even though the *pearl* is gone; this lamentable error clings hard to many a man.

112. And thus it happens that the *tree of pearl* in the *garden of Christ* is often spoiled; concerning which the *Scripture* makes a *hard knot* or *conclusion*, *namely, that those who have once tasted the sweetness of the world to come, and fall away from it again, shall hardly see the kingdom of God.*

113. And though it cannot be denied that the *gates of grace* still stand open, yet the false and dazzling *light* of the outward *reason* of the soul so deceives and hinders such men that they suppose they have the *pearl*, while they yet live for the vanity of this world and dance with the *devil* to his pipe.

---

# Chapter V.

### How a man may call himself a Christian, and how not.

HERE therefore a Christian should consider *why* he calls himself a *Christian*, and examine truly whether he is one or not. For surely my learning to *know* and confess that I am a *sinner*, and that *Christ* has destroyed my *sins* on the *cross* and shed His *blood* for me, does not make me a *Christian*.

115. The *inheritance* belongs only to the *children*. A maidservant in a house knows well enough what the mistress wants to have done, and yet that does not make her the *heiress* of her mistress's goods. The very *devils know* that there is a

<summary>The author warns that prioritizing worldly pleasure and reason over the inward spirit leads to the loss of spiritual enlightenment, and argues that true Christianity requires more than just intellectual belief.</summary>
<keywords>inward man, outward man, pearl, reason, hypocrisy, true Christianity, spiritual inheritance, vanity</keywords>

--- Page 102 ---
<meta>This page continues the comparison between the inward spirit and the outward man, specifically addressing the requirement for spiritual rebirth to become a true heir of Christ's kingdom.</meta>

Of Regeneration.

GOD, yet that does not *change* them into *Angels* again. But if the maidservant in the house shall be *married* to the *son* of her mistress, then she may come to *inherit* her mistress’s goods. And so it is to be understood also in the matter of being a *Christian*.

116. The children of the *history* are not the *heirs* of the goods of *Christ*, but the *legitimate children, regenerated by the Spirit of Christ*, are the only true *heirs*. For GOD said to *Abraham: "Cast out the son of the bondwoman, for he shall not inherit with the son of the free woman."* For he was a *scorner*, and only an *historical son* of the *faith* and *spirit of Abraham*; and as long as he continued to be such a person, he was not a true *inheritor* of the *faith* of *Abraham*. Therefore, GOD commanded that he should be *cast out* from *inheriting* his goods; this was a *type* <note>a symbolic foreshadowing</note> of the *Christendom* which was to come.

117. For the *promise of Christendom* was made to *Abraham*; therefore the *type* was then also set forth by two brothers, *Isaac* and *Ishmael*, in order to show how *Christendom* would behave *itself*, and that two sorts of men would be in it, *namely*, true *Christians* and *lip-Christians*. The latter, under the title of *Christianity*, would be nothing but mockers, as *Ishmael* was, and also *Esau*, who was a *type of the outward Adam*, just as *Jacob* was a *type of Christ* and *His true Christendom*.

118. Thus everyone who will call himself a *Christian* must *cast out* from himself the *son of the bondwoman*—that is, the *earthly will*—and be forever killing and destroying it, and not let it settle into the *inheritance*.

119. Nor should we give the *pearl* to the *bestial man* for *him* to amuse himself with in the *outward light*, in the *lust of the flesh*. Instead, we must, with our father *Abraham*, bring the *son of the right will* to *Mount Moriah* and be ready in obedience to GOD to offer it up, always in *will* dying from *sin* through the *death of Christ*. We must give no place to the *beast of vanity* in the *kingdom of Christ*, nor suffer it to grow wanton, proud, covetous, envious, and malicious. For all these are the properties of *Ishmael, the son of the bondwoman*, whom *Adam* begat in his vanity on the wanton whore, the false *bondwoman*, by the *devil’s imagination*, out of the *earthly property in flesh and blood*.

120. This *mocker* and *titular Christian* is the *son of the false bondwoman* and must be cast out; for he shall not possess the *inheritance of Christ* in the kingdom of GOD. He is not fit; he is only *Babel*, a confusion of that *one language* into many *languages*. He is only a *talker* and a *wrangler* about the *inheritance*; he intends to get it for himself by *talking* and *wrangling*, through the hypocrisy of his lips and a pretense of holiness, although he is

<summary>The author explains that true Christians are those regenerated by the Spirit, distinguishing them from "lip-Christians" or "historical" believers. Using the biblical types of Isaac and Ishmael, he argues that the earthly will must be cast out and sacrificed to God to inherit Christ's kingdom.</summary>
<keywords>Regeneration, Abraham, Isaac, Ishmael, Lip-Christians, Earthly Will, Inheritance, Babel, Mount Moriah, Bestial Man</keywords>

--- Page 103 ---
<meta>This page continues the argument that outward profession of faith is insufficient without the inward practice of virtue and the actual experience of being born again through the Spirit of Christ.</meta>

no better than a bloodthirsty *murderer* of his brother *Abel*, who is the *right heir*.

121. Therefore we say what we *know*: that he who calls himself a *true Christian* must *test* himself and find what kind of *qualities* drive and rule him; whether the *Spirit of Christ* moves him toward *truth* and *righteousness*, and toward the *love* of his *neighbor*, so that he would willingly do what is right if he only knew how.

122. Now, if he finds that he has a real *hunger* for such *virtue*, then he may justly think that he is *drawn*. And then he must begin to *practice* accordingly, and not be content with a *will* alone, without *doing*. The *drawing of the Father to Christ* consists in the *will*, but the true *life* consists in the *doing*; for the *right spirit does* that which is *right*.

123. But if there is the *will to do*, and yet the *doing* does not follow, then the *true man* is still shut up in vain lust, which suppresses the *doing*. Therefore such a person is but a *hypocrite* and an *Ishmaelite*; he speaks one thing and does another, and witnesses by this that his mouth is a *liar*; for he himself does not do that which he *teaches*, and consequently only serves the bestial man in *vanity*.

124. For he who says, "*I have a will, and would willingly do good, but the earthly flesh which I carry about me keeps me back so that I cannot; yet I shall be saved by grace, for the merits of Christ. I comfort myself with His merit and sufferings, for He will receive me by mere grace, without any merits of my own, and forgive me my sins*." Such a person, I say, is like a man who knows what food is good for his health, yet will not eat of it, but eats *poison* instead, from which *sickness* and *death* will certainly follow.

125. For what good does it do the soul to *know* the *way to God* if it will not *walk* in it, but goes on in a contrary path? What good will it do the soul to *comfort* itself with the *sonship* <note>the state of being a child of God</note> of Christ, with *His passion* and *death*, and so flatter itself with the hopes of getting the *inheritance* by those means, if it will not *enter* into the *filial birth* <note>being born as a child of God</note>, so that it may be a *true child*, born out of the *spirit of Christ*, out of *His suffering, death,* and *resurrection*? Surely, *pleasing* and *flattering* oneself with *Christ's merits*, without the *true innate sonship*, is *falsehood* and a *lie*, whoever he may be who teaches it.

126. *This comfort* belongs only to the *penitent sinner* who strives against *sin* and the *anger of God*. When temptations come, and the *devil* assaults such a poor *repentant soul*, then it must wholly wrap itself up in the *merits and death of Christ* as its sole armor of defense.

<summary>The author argues that a true Christian must demonstrate their faith through righteous actions and a sincere hunger for virtue rather than mere words. Relying on Christ's merits without undergoing a true spiritual "filial birth" is dismissed as a dangerous hypocrisy.</summary>
<keywords>true Christian, Spirit of Christ, practice of virtue, hypocrisy, Ishmaelite, merits of Christ, sonship, filial birth, repentance, way to God</keywords>

--- Page 104 ---
<meta>This page continues the argument that salvation is not merely a legalistic "imputed righteousness" but a literal internal transformation, shifting focus from outward profession to the necessity of the New Birth.</meta>

# Of Regeneration.

127. *Christ alone* indeed has *merited* redemption for us; but not in such a way that for the sake of *His* own proper *merit*, He will freely grant us *His <term>childship</term>* by an *outward adoption* only, and so receive us as His *children* when we are not. No. *He Himself* is the *merit*; He is the open *gate* that leads through *death*; and through that *gate* we must enter. He receives no "beast" into His *merit*, but only those who *turn* and become like *children*. Those children who thus come to Him are His reward, which He has merited.

128. For He said, "Father, the men were Yours and You have given them to me," [as my reward] "and I will give them eternal life." But the *life of Christ* will be given to no one unless they come to Him in His *Spirit*, into His *humanity*, *sufferings*, and *merit*, and are *born therein as true children of the merit*.

129. We must *be born of His merit* and put on the *merit of Christ* in His *passion and death*; not *outwardly* with verbal *flattery* only, or by simply comforting ourselves with it while we still remain *aliens* and *foreign children*, of a foreign essence or *nature*. No, the foreign essence does not *inherit* the *childship*, but the *innate essence inherits* it.

130. This *innate* essence is not *of this world*, but *in heaven*, of which *St. Paul* speaks, saying, *Our citizenship is in heaven*. The *filial* essence walks *in heaven*, and *heaven* is in man.

131. But if *heaven* in man is not *open*, and the man stands outside *heaven* flattering himself, saying, "*I am still outside, but Christ will take me in through His grace; is His merit not mine?*" Such a one exists in *vanity* and *sin* with the *outward man*, and with the *soul in hell, that is*, in the *anger of* GOD.

132. Therefore, learn to understand rightly what *Christ* has taught us and done for us. *He* is our *heaven*; He must take *form* in us, or else we shall not be in *heaven*. Thus, the soul’s *inward man*, with the *holy body of Christ, that is*, in the *new birth*, is in *heaven*, and the *outward mortal man* is in the *world*, of which *Christ* spoke, saying, "My sheep are in my hand, and no one shall snatch them away; the Father who gave them to me is greater than all."

->The Way to Christ.<-

# Chapter VI.

## Concerning the Right and Wrong Way of Going to Church, Receiving the Sacraments, and Absolution.

BELOVED Brethren, we will teach you *faithfully*, not with flattering lips to please the *Antichrist*, but from our *Pearl*, the *virtue*, *power*, and *spirit* of *Christ* in us, from a *Christian essence* and *knowledge*; not from the *husk* and *history*, but from a *newborn spirit*, from *Christ's knowledge*, as a *branch* growing on the *vine, Christ*; from the *measure* of that *knowledge* which is opened in us, according to the will and counsel of God.

134. Today, people tie us to the *history* and to the physical *churches of stone*; these churches are indeed good in their own way, if people also brought the *temple of Christ* into them. They teach, moreover, that *their absolution* is a *forgiving of sins*, and that the *Supper of the Lord* takes away *sin*; also, that the *Spirit of God* comes into people through *their ministry*. All of this has a proper meaning, if it were rightly understood, and if people did not cling merely to the *husk*.

135. Many a person goes to *church* for twenty or thirty years, hears *sermons*, receives the *sacraments*, and hears *absolution* read or declared, and yet is as much a *beast* of the *devil* and *vanity* at the last as at the first. A *beast* goes into the *church* and to the *Supper*, and a *beast* comes out of them again.

136. How will he *eat* who has no *mouth*? Can any person *eat* that food which is so shut up that he cannot reach it? How will he *drink* if he can reach no *water*? Or how will he *hear* if he has no *hearing*?

137. What good purpose does it serve for me to go to physical *churches of stone* and there fill my ears with empty *breath*? Or to go to the *Supper* and feed nothing but the earthly *mouth*, which is mortal and corruptible? Cannot I feed and satisfy that with a piece of bread at home? What good does it do for the soul, which is an immortal life, to have the *animal man* observe the *form* and venerate the *shell* of *Christ’s* institution, if it cannot obtain the *kernel* of it? For St. Paul says of the *Supper*:
> ‘You receive it to condemnation, because you do not discern the Lord’s body.’

138. The covenant stands firm and *is active* in the use of the institution. *Christ* offers His *spirit* to us in His *word* (*namely*, in His preached *word*) and His body and blood in the

<summary>Boehme argues that outward religious rituals like attending church, receiving the sacraments, and hearing absolution are useless unless the participant possesses an internal, spiritual connection to Christ. He warns that participating in the Lord's Supper without spiritual discernment leads to condemnation rather than salvation.</summary>
<keywords>Church, Sacraments, Absolution, Lord's Supper, St. Paul, Spiritual Discernment, Ritualism, Newborn Spirit</keywords>

--- Page 106 ---
<meta>This page continues Chapter VI, specifically addressing why external church rituals like absolution and the sacraments are ineffective for those who lack an inward spiritual capacity.</meta>

# Of Regeneration.

*Sacrament*, and His *Absolution* in a *brotherly reconciliation* one to another.

139. But what good does it do for a *beast* to stand and listen, if it has no *hearing* to receive the *inward living Word*, nor any *ground* in which to lay the *Word* so that it may bring forth *fruit*? Of such people *Christ* says, "The Devil plucks the Word out of their hearts, lest they should believe and be saved." But how can he do so? Because the *Word* finds no *place* in the *hearing mind* to take *root*.

140. And it is the same with *absolution* also: what benefit is it to me for someone to say, *I pronounce or declare to you the forgiveness of your sins*, when my soul is wholly *shut up* in sin? Whoever says this to a *sinner* so *shut up* errs; and he who receives it without the *Voice of God* within himself confirming it deceives himself. *None can forgive sins but God alone.*

141. The *preacher* does not have the *forgiveness of sins* in his *own power*; rather, it is the *Spirit of Christ* in the *voice* of the priest that has the power, provided the priest himself is a *Christian*.

142. What good did it do for those who heard *Christ* himself teaching on Earth, when he said, "Come unto me, all you who are weary and heavy laden, and I will give you rest"? What good did this blessed *promise* do for those who heard it, if they did not labor nor were *heavy laden*? What became of the *refreshment* or *rest* then? Since they had *dead ears* and heard only the *outward Christ*—and not the *Word* of the *Divine Power*—certainly they were not *refreshed*. The *bestial man* receives exactly that much benefit from his *absolution* and *sacraments*.

143. The *covenant* is *open* in the *sacraments*; and in the *office* or *ministry* of teaching, the *covenant* is also stirred. The soul does receive it, but only in *that property* which matches the *mouth* of the soul.

144. That is, the *outward beast* receives *bread* and *wine*, which it might just as well have at home. And the *fiery soul* receives the *Testament* according to its *property*; namely, in the *anger* of God it receives the *substance* of the *eternal world*, but according to the *property* of the *dark world*. It receives it, therefore, as the *Scripture* says, *to its own judgment or condemnation*. For as the *mouth* is, so is the *food* taken in by the mouth. And it is in this same way that the *wicked* shall behold *Christ* at the *last judgment* as a *severe judge*, but the *saints* shall behold him as a *loving Immanuel*.

145. God's *anger* stands open in his *Testaments* toward the *wicked*; but toward the *saints*, his *heavenly loving-kindness* stands open, and in it the *power of Christ* in the *Holy Name Jesus*,

<summary>Boehme explains that external religious rites like absolution and the sacraments require an inward spiritual "hearing" to be effective; without a soul prepared by God, the recipient remains unchanged or even receives judgment. <meta>This page continues the discussion on the inward nature of spiritual life, contrasting the true reception of the sacraments and preaching with the hollow, external practices of the ungodly.</meta>

stands open. What good then does the *Holy Thing* do for the *Wicked*, who cannot enjoy it? Or what is there that can *take away* his *sins*, when his *sin* is only stirred and made manifest by it?

146. The *Sacraments* do not *take away sin*; nor are *sins forgiven* through them. But it is like this: when *Christ* arises, then *Adam* dies in the *essence* of the *serpent*; just as when the *sun* rises, the *night* is swallowed up in the *day*, and the *night* is no more. Just so are *sins forgiven*.

147. The *Spirit* of *Christ* eats of his *Holy Substance*; the *inward man* is the *receiver* of the *Holy Substance*. He receives what the *Spirit* of *Christ* brings into him—namely, the *Temple* of God, *Christ's Flesh* and *Blood*. But what does this concern a *beast*? Or what does it concern the *devils*? Or the *soul* that is in the *wrath* (Anger) of God? These eat of the *heavenly blood* that is in the *heaven* where *they* dwell, which is the *abyss* or bottomless *pit*.

148. And it is also the same in the office or ministry of *preaching*: the *ungodly man* hears what the *outward soul* of the *outward world* preaches. That he receives—namely, the *history*. And if there is *straw* or *stubble* in what is taught, he sucks the *vanity* out of it. Indeed, if the preaching is mere calumny, railing, and uncharitable abuse—as is sometimes the case—then his soul sucks the *venomous poison* and the *murdering cruelty* of the *devil* from it. He tickles himself with it and is pleased with learning how to judge and condemn others.

149. Thus, if the *preacher* is one who is *dead* and has no *true life* in him, but sows only *venom* and *reproach* proceeding out of his evil affections, then it is the *devil* who *teaches* and the *devil* who *hears*. Such teaching is received into a *wicked heart* and brings forth *wicked fruits*. By this means, the *world* has become a mere *den* of *murdering devils*. So, if you look among the herd of such *teachers* and *hearers*, there is little to be found but *revilings*, *slanderings*, and *reproachings*, together with *contention about words* and *wrangling about the husk*.

150. But the *Holy Spirit* teaches in the *holy teacher*, and the *Spirit* of *Christ* hears through the soul, which is the *divine house* of the *divine sound* or *voice* in the *holy hearer*.

151. The *holy man* has his *church within himself*, where he *hears* and *teaches*. But *Babel* has a *heap of stones* into which she goes with her *seeming holiness* and *real hypocrisy*. There, she loves to be seen in fine *clothes* and makes a very devout and godly show. The *church of stone* is her *god*, in which she puts her *confidence*.

<summary>The author explains that sacraments and preaching only benefit the spiritually "inward man," while the wicked merely absorb vanity or malice from external rituals. True spiritual life is an internal reality, contrasting with the "Babel" of outward religious hypocrisy and physical church buildings.</summary>
<keywords>regeneration, sacraments, preaching, inward man, hypocrisy, Babel, Holy Spirit, forgiveness of sins, spiritual substance</keywords>

--- Page 108 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the internal versus external nature of worship, emphasizing that a holy person carries the church within themselves regardless of their physical location.</meta>

# Of Regeneration.

152. But the *holy* man has his *church* around him everywhere, even in *himself*; for he always stands and walks, sits and lies down in his *church*. He lives in the true Christian church; yes, in the *temple* of *Christ*; the *Holy Spirit* preaches to him out of every *creature*. Whatever he looks upon, he sees a preacher of GOD in it.
153. Now here the *scoffer* will say that I despise the *church of stone* where the congregation meets; but I say that I do not. For I only expose the hypocritical *Whore of Babylon*, who commits *prostitution* with the *church of stone* and calls herself a *Christian*, but is indeed a *harlot*.
154. A true *Christian* brings his *holy church* with him into the congregation. For the *heart* is the true *church*, where a person must practice the *service of God*. If I should go a thousand <unclear>times</unclear> to *church*, and to the *sacrament* every week, and hear *absolution* declared to me every day, and do not have *Christ in me*, all would be false—an unprofitable fiction and a carved image in *Babel*, and no forgiving of sins.
155. A *holy* man does *holy* works from the *holy strength* of his mind. The *work* is not the *atonement of reconciliation*, but it is the *building* which the true *Spirit builds* in his *substance*; it is his *habitation*. But the fiction and fancy is the *habitation of the false Christian*, into which his soul enters with deceit. The *outward hearing* reaches only to the *outward*, and works in the *outward* only; but the *inward hearing* goes into the *inward*, and works in the *inward*.
156. Pretend, roar, cry, sing, preach, and teach as much as you will; yet if your *inward teacher* and *hearer* are not *open*, all is nothing but a *Babel*, a fiction, and a carved image, by which the *spirit of the outward world* models and makes for itself a carved image in resemblance of the *inward*; and it makes a *holy show* with it, as if it performed some *divine* or *holy service* to GOD. Whereas many times in such service and worship, the *devil* works mightily in the *imagination*, and very much tickles the *heart* with those things in which the *flesh* delights. This indeed happens often to the *children of God*, in their *outward man*, if they do not take great heed; so busily does the *devil* beset and sift them.

<summary>The author argues that true worship occurs within the heart, which is the real church, rather than through external rituals or physical buildings. Without an internal spiritual awakening, outward religious acts are mere fictions or "carved images" that can be exploited by the devil.</summary>
<keywords>regeneration, internal church, temple of Christ, Holy Spirit, Whore of Babylon, heart, service of God, Babel, inward teacher, imagination, spiritual hypocrisy</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the internal nature of true faith, moving from the critique of external rituals to the internal peace and non-sectarian nature of a person born of the Spirit.</meta>

# Chapter VII.

## Of Unprofitable Opinions, and Strife about the Letter.

158. A TRUE Christian, who is *born anew* of the *Spirit of Christ*, is in the *simplicity of Christ*, and has no *strife* or *contention* with any man about *religion*. He has *strife* enough in *himself*, with his own *bestial evil flesh and blood*. He continually thinks himself a great sinner and is afraid of GOD; but the *love of Christ* by degrees pierces through and expels that *fear*, as the *day* swallows up the *night*.

159. But the *sins* of the impenitent *man* rest in the *sleep of death*, bud forth in the *pit*, and produce their *fruit* in *hell*.

160. The *Christendom* that is in *Babel* strives about the *manner* how men ought to serve GOD and glorify *Him*; also how they are to *know* Him, and what *He* is in *His essence and will*. And they preach positively that whoever is not one and the same with them in every particular of *knowledge* and *opinion* is no *Christian*, but a *heretic*.

161. Now I would gladly see how all their *sects* can be brought to agree in *that one* which might be called a *true Christian Church*; when all of them are *scorners*, every *party* of them reviling the rest and proclaiming them to be false.

162. But a *Christian* is of no *sect*; he can dwell in the midst of *sects* and appear in their *services* without being attached or bound to any. He has but one *knowledge*, and that is *Christ in him*. He seeks but one *way*, which is the *desire* always to do and teach that which is *right*; and he puts all his *knowing* and *willing* into the *life of Christ*.

163. He sighs and wishes continually that the *will of GOD* might be done in him, and that His *kingdom* might be manifested in him. He daily and hourly kills *sin* in the *flesh*; for the *seed of the woman, namely, the inward man in Christ*, continually breaks the *head* of the *serpent*, that is, the *power of the devil*, which is in *vanity*.

164. His *faith* is a *desire after God and goodness*; which he wraps up in a sure *hope*, trusting to the *words of the promise*, and lives and dies therein; though as to the *true man*, he never dies.

165. For *Christ* says, "Whoever believes in me shall never die, but has pierced through from *death to life*; and *rivers* of *living water* shall flow from him," *namely*, good doctrine and works.

<summary>This chapter argues that a true Christian avoids sectarian disputes and intellectual pride, focusing instead on the internal presence of Christ and the daily struggle against personal sin. It contrasts the unified life of a believer with the confusion and judgmental nature of the divided "Babel" of institutional Christendom.</summary>
<keywords>Spirit of Christ, non-sectarianism, internal struggle, Babel, true Christian Church, seed of the woman, spiritual regeneration, faith</keywords>

--- Page 110 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the internal nature of true faith, contrasting the unity of the Spirit with the confusion and strife found in "Babel" (sectarian religious disputes).</meta>

# Of Regeneration.

166. Therefore I say, that whatever fights and contends about the *Letter* is all *Babel*. The *Letters* of the *Word* proceed from and all stand in one *Root*, which is the *Spirit of God*; just as the various *Flowers* all stand in the *Earth* and grow alongside one another. They do not fight with each other about their difference of *Color, Smell*, and *Taste*, but allow the *Earth*, the *Sun*, the *Rain*, the *Wind*, the *Heat* and *Cold* to do with them as they please; and yet every one of them grows in its own peculiar *Essence* and *Property*.

167. Even so it is with the *Children* of *GOD*; they have various *Gifts* and *Degrees of Knowledge*, yet all from one *Spirit*. They all rejoice at the great *Wonders of GOD*, and give Thanks to the most *High* in His *Wisdom*. Why then should they contend about Him in whom they live and have their *Being*, and of whose *Substance* they themselves are?

168. It is the greatest *Folly* that is in *Babel* for *People* to strive about *Religion*, as the *Devil* has made the *World* do; so that they contend vehemently about *Opinions* of their own *Forging*, namely, about the *Letter*; when the *Kingdom of God* consists in no *Opinion*, but in *Power* and *Love*.

169. As *Christ* said to his *Disciples*, and left it with them at the last, saying, ‘*Love* one another, as I have loved you; for thereby *Men* shall know that you are my *Disciples*.’ If *Men* would as fervently seek after *Love* and *Righteousness* as they do after *Opinions*, there would be no *Strife* on *Earth*, and we should be as *Children* of *One Father*, and should need no *Law* or *Ordinance*.

170. For *GOD* is not served by any *Law*, but only by *Obedience*. *Laws* are for the *Wicked*, who will not embrace *Love* and *Righteousness*; they are, and must be, compelled and forced by *Laws*.

171. We all have but one single *Order*, *Law*, or *Ordinance*, which is to stand still to the *Lord* of all *Beings*, and resign our *Wills* up to Him, and allow His *Spirit* to play what *Music* He will. And thus we give to Him again as *His own Fruits* that which He works and manifests in us.

172. Now if we did not contend about our different *Fruits*, *Gifts*, *Kinds* and *Degrees of Knowledge*, but did acknowledge them in one another, like *Children* of the *Spirit of God*, what could condemn us? For the *Kingdom of God* consists not in our knowing and supposing, but in *Power*.

173. If we did not know half so much, and were more like *Children*, and had but a *Brotherly Mind* and *good Will* towards one another, and lived like *Children* of one *Mother*, and as *Branches* of one *Tree*, taking our *Sap* all from one *Root*, we should be far more *Holy* than we are.

<summary>The author argues that religious conflict over literal interpretations and opinions is a sign of spiritual confusion, whereas true children of God live in unified diversity like flowers in a garden. He emphasizes that the Kingdom of God is found in love, power, and resignation to the Divine Will rather than in dogmatic laws or intellectual knowledge.</summary>
<keywords>Regeneration, Babel, Spirit of God, Religious Strife, Unity in Diversity, Kingdom of God, Love, Obedience, Resignation, Spiritual Knowledge</keywords>

--- Page 111 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the nature of spiritual unity and the futility of religious strife, contrasting the true knowledge found in the Spirit with the contentious behavior of those who argue over doctrine.</meta>

174. *Knowledge* serves only this purpose: namely, to *know* that we have lost the *Divine Power* in *Adam* and have now become inclined to *Sin*; that we have evil *properties* within us, and that doing *evil* does not please GOD; so that through our *knowledge* we might learn to do *right*. Now, if we have the *power* of GOD within us and desire with all our hearts to act and live aright, then our *knowledge* is merely our *delight*, or a matter of pleasure in which we rejoice.

175. For true *knowledge* is the *manifestation* of the *Spirit of God* through *eternal Wisdom*. He *knows* what He wills in *His children*; He shows His *wisdom* and *wonders* through His *children*, just as the earth puts forth *its* various flowers.

176. Now, if we dwell with one another like humble *children* in the *Spirit of Christ*, each one rejoicing in the gift and knowledge of the other, who would *judge* or *condemn* us? Who *judges* or condemns the birds in the woods, which praise the *Lord* of all beings with *various* voices, each one in its own *essence*? Does the *Spirit of God* reprove them for not bringing their voices into *one* harmony? Does not their *melody* all proceed from His power, and do they not *play* before Him?

177. Therefore, those men who *strive* and wrangle about the *knowledge* and *will of God*, and despise one another on that account, are more *foolish* than the birds in the woods and the wild beasts that have no true understanding. They are more *unprofitable* in the sight of the holy GOD than the flowers of the field, which stand still in quiet submission to the *Spirit of God* and allow Him to manifest *Divine Wisdom* and *power* through them. Indeed, such men are *worse* than *thistles* and *thorns* that grow among beautiful flowers, *for they at least stand still and are quiet*, whereas those wranglers are like *ravenous beasts* and *birds of prey*, which *frighten* the other birds from singing and praising GOD.

178. In short, they are the *offspring*, *branches*, or *sprouts* of the *devil* in the *anger* of GOD, who, nevertheless, must be made to serve the LORD through their very *tormenting*; for by their plaguing and persecuting, they press out the *sap* through the *essence of the children of God* so that they move and stir themselves in the *Spirit of God* with prayer and continual sighing, in which exercise of their powers the *Spirit of God* moves Himself within them.

179. For through this the *desire* is exerted, and so the *children of God* grow green, flourish, and bring forth fruit; for the *children of God* are *revealed* in *tribulation*, as the *Scripture* says, "When you chastise them, they cry fervently to you."

<summary>The author explains that true spiritual knowledge should lead to righteous living rather than contention, comparing those who argue over doctrine to predatory beasts that disrupt the natural praise of God. He suggests that even the persecution from such people serves to strengthen the faith and growth of God's true children.</summary>
<keywords>Knowledge, Divine Power, Spirit of God, eternal Wisdom, Spirit of Christ, Essence, Melody, Tribulation, persecution, religious strife</keywords>

Of Regeneration.

## Chapter VIII.

### Wherein Christian Religion consists, and how Men should serve God and their Brethren.

ALL Christian religion wholly consists in this: to learn to know ourselves—from where we have come and what we are; how we have gone forth from the *Unity* into *Dissension*, *Wickedness*, and *Unrighteousness*; how we have awakened and stirred up these evils in us; and how we may be delivered from them again, and recover our original blessedness.

181. First, how we were in the *Unity*, when we were the *children* of God in *Adam* before he fell. Secondly, how we are now in *Dissension* and *Disunion*, in *Strife* and *Contrariety*. Thirdly, where we go when we pass out of this corruptible condition; where with the *immortal* part, and where with the mortal part.

182. And lastly, how we may come forth from *Disunion* and *Vanity*, and enter again into that *one Tree*, *Christ in us*, out of which we all sprang in *Adam*. In these *four points* all the necessary *knowledge* of a Christian consists.

183. So that we need not *strive* about anything; we have no cause for *contention* with each other. Let everyone only exercise himself in learning how he may enter again into the *love of God and his brother*.

184. The *Testaments of Christ* are nothing else but a *loving bond* or *brotherly covenant*, with which GOD in *Christ* binds himself to us and us to him. All teaching, willing, living, and doing, must imply, aim at, and refer to *that*. All teaching and doing otherwise, whatever it may be, is *Babel* and a *fiction*; a mere graven image of *pride* in unprofitable judgments, a disturbing of the world, and a hypocrisy of the *Devil*, with which he blinds *simplicity*.
<margin>•</margin>
185. Every preacher void of the *Spirit of God*, who without *Divine knowledge* sets himself up as a *teacher of divine things*, pretending to serve GOD thereby, is false, and does nothing but *serve the belly*, his idol, and his own proud insolent *mind*, in desiring to be honored on that account, and esteemed *holy*, or a *divine in holy orders*. He bears an *office* to which he is set apart and chosen by the *children of men*, who do nothing but flatter him, and for favor have *ordained* him thereunto.

186. *Christ* said, "Whoever does not enter by the door," that is, through his Spirit, "into the sheepfold, but climbs up some

<summary>This chapter defines true Christian religion as the pursuit of self-knowledge and the return to divine unity in Christ, contrasting this with the vanity of religious strife. It warns against prideful preachers who lack the Spirit of God and prioritize human recognition over divine service.</summary>

"other way, the same is a thief and a murderer, and the sheep
do not follow him, for they do not know his voice."

187. He does not have the *Voice* of the *Spirit of GOD*, but the *Voice*
of *his own art* and *learning* only; the *man* teaches, and not
the *Spirit of GOD*. But *Christ* says, "Every plant which my
heavenly Father has not planted shall be plucked up by the
roots."

188. How then will he who is *ungodly* plant *heavenly plants*,
when he has no *seed* alive in its *power in himself*? *Christ* says
expressly, "The sheep do not hear his voice; they do not follow him."

189. The *written Word* is but an *instrument* by which the
*Spirit* leads us to *itself within us*. That *Word* which will
teach must be *living in the literal Word*. The *Spirit of GOD*
must be in the *literal sound*, or else no one is a *teacher* of GOD,
but a mere *teacher* of the *letter*, a *knower* of the *history*, and
not of the *Spirit of GOD in Christ*.

190. All that men will serve GOD with must be done *in*
*faith, namely*, in the *Spirit*. It is the *Spirit* that makes the
work perfect and acceptable in the sight of GOD. All that a
man undertakes and does in *faith*, he does in the *Spirit of*
*GOD*, which *Spirit* cooperates in the work, and then
it is acceptable to GOD. For *He has done it Himself*, and His
*power* and *virtue* are in it: It is *holy*.

191. But whatever is done in *self*, without *faith*, is but a
*figure* and *shell*, or *husk*, of a *true Christian work*.

192. If you serve your brother and do it only in *hypocrisy*,
and *give to him unwillingly*, then you do not serve God. For your
*faith* does not proceed from *love*, nor does it enter into *hope* in
your gift. Indeed you *serve* your brother, and he for his part
thanks GOD and *blesses* you, but you do not *bless* him. For
you give him your gift with a *grudging spirit*, which does not enter
into the *spirit of GOD*, into the *hope of faith*; therefore your
gift is but *half* given, and you have but *half* your reward for it.

193. The same is true of *receiving* a gift. If *anyone gives in*
*faith*, in divine *hope*, he blesses his gift by his *faith*: But
whoever *receives it unthankfully* and murmurs in his spirit, he
*curses* it in the use or enjoyment of it. Thus it is that every
one shall have his own; "Whatever he sows, that shall he
also reap."

194. So likewise it is in the *office of teaching*; "whatever a
man sows, that also he reaps." For if any man sows good
seed from the *spirit of Christ*, it sticks in the *good heart* and
brings forth *good fruit*; but in the *wicked*, who are not capable
of receiving the *good seed*, the *anger of GOD* is stirred.

195. If any sow contentions, reproaches, and misconstruc-

# Of Regeneration.

...tions, all *ungodly* people receive *that* unto themselves; which sticks in *them* also, and brings forth *fruit* accordingly. So they learn from it to despise, revile, slander, and misrepresent one another. Out of this root the *great Babel* has sprung and grown; wherein men, out of mere *pride* and *strife*, contend about the *history* and the *justification* of a poor *sinner* in the sight of GOD; thereby causing the simple to stray and blaspheme, so much that one brother reviles and curses the other, and excommunicates, or casts him to the *Devil*, for the sake of the *history* and *letter*.

196. Such *railers* and *revilers* do not fear GOD, but raise the great *building of dissension*. And seeing that corrupt lust lies in all men, in the *earthly flesh* still, they raise and awaken *abominations* even in the *simple children of God*, and make the people of GOD, as well as the children of iniquity, blaspheme. And thus they become *master-builders* of the great *Babel* of the world, and are as useful in the church as a *fifth wheel* on a *wagon*; indeed, what is worse than that, they erect the *hellish building* too.

197. Therefore it is highly necessary for the *children of God* to pray earnestly, that they may learn to *know* this *false building*, and depart from it with their minds, and not help to build it up and persecute their *fellow-children of God*. For by that means they keep *themselves* back from the *heavenly kingdom*, and turn aside from the right way.

198. According to the saying of *Christ* to the *Pharisees*, "Woe unto you, Pharisees; for you travel over sea and land to make one proselyte, and when he is one, you make him twice as much the child of hell as yourselves." Which is truly too much the case with the *modern factions* and *sects* among these criers and teachers of *strife*.

199. I desire therefore, out of my *gifts* which are *revealed* to me from GOD, that all the *children of God*, who desire to be the *true members of Christ*, be faithfully warned to depart from such abominable *contentions* and bloody *firebrands*, and to go forth from all *strife* with their brethren, and *strive* only after *love* and *righteousness* toward all men.

200. For he who is a *good tree* must bring forth *good fruits*, and must sometimes suffer *swine* to devour his *fruits*, and yet must continue to be *a good tree* still, and be always willing to work with GOD, and not suffer any *evil* to overcome him. And then he stands and grows in the *field of GOD*, and brings forth *fruit* to be set upon GOD's *table*, which he shall enjoy *forever*. Amen. "Let all that have breath praise the name of the Lord. Hallelujah."

<summary>The author warns against religious sectarianism and "master-builders of Babel" who prioritize intellectual history and strife over spiritual love. True children of God are encouraged to avoid these contentions and instead produce the "good fruit" of righteousness and patience.</summary>

### THE
# FOURTH BOOK.

---

### A
## DIALOGUE
### BETWEEN A
->*Scholar and his Master.*<-
->Concerning the<-
## SUPER-SENSUAL LIFE.
### SHOWING

How the Soul may attain to *Divine Hearing* and *Vision*, and what its *Childship* in the *Natural* and *Supernatural Life* is; and how it passes out of *Nature* into *God*, and out of *God* into *Nature* and *Self* again; also what its *Salvation* and *Perdition* are.

---

->Written in the *German Language*,<-
->(In the year 1624.)<-
## By *JACOB* BOEHME.

<summary>This title page introduces the fourth book of Jacob Boehme's works, consisting of a dialogue between a Scholar and a Master regarding the super-sensual life. It outlines themes of divine vision, the soul's relationship with nature and God, and the definitions of salvation and perdition.</summary>

<meta>This page serves as a scriptural preface to "The Fourth Book," grounding the upcoming dialogue between the Scholar and the Master in the apostolic teachings of St. Paul regarding spiritual versus worldly wisdom.</meta>

*WE speak the hidden mystical wisdom of God, which God ordained before the world for our glory; which none of the princes of this world knew. For had they known it, they would not have crucified the Lord of Glory. But, as it is written: eye has not seen, nor ear heard, nor has it entered into the heart of man to conceive the things which God has prepared for those who love him. But God has revealed them to us by his Spirit; for the Spirit searches all things, even the deep things of God. For what man knows the things of a man except the spirit of a man which is in him? Even so, no one knows the things of God except the Spirit of God. Now, we have received not the spirit of the world, but the Spirit which is from God, so that we might know the things that are freely given to us by God. These things we also speak, not in the words which man's wisdom teaches, but which the Holy Spirit teaches, comparing spiritual things with spiritual. But the natural man does not receive the things of the Spirit of God, for they are foolishness to him; nor can he know them, because they are spiritually discerned. But he who is spiritual judges, or discerns, all things. — 1 Corinthians 2:7–15.*

<summary>This page quotes 1 Corinthians 2:7–15 to establish that divine wisdom is a hidden mystery revealed only through the Holy Spirit. it contrasts the "natural man," who lacks spiritual insight, with the spiritual person who is capable of discerning God's truths.</summary>
